        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_double), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
        ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int),
    ]
    _lib.sl_mpc_get_shield_branches.restype = ctypes.c_int


class CppCurvilinearSLMPC:
    """
    High-Performance Native C++ Curvilinear SL-MPC Planner.
    100% Drop-In Compatible with CurvilinearSLMPC.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        costmap_engine: any = None,
        horizon_N: int = MPC_HORIZON_N,
        dt: float = MPC_DT,
        q_progress: float = MPC_Q_PROGRESS,
        q_cte: float = MPC_Q_CTE,
        q_heading: float = MPC_Q_HEADING,
        r_steer: float = MPC_R_STEER,
        r_accel: float = MPC_R_ACCEL,
        r_steer_rate: float = MPC_R_STEER_RATE,
        mu_friction: float = MPC_MU_FRICTION,
        v_corner_factor: float = MPC_V_CORNER_FACTOR,
        v_cruise_max: float = MPC_V_CRUISE_MAX,
        v_cruise_min: float = MPC_V_CRUISE_MIN,
        alt_switch_hysteresis: float = MPC_ALT_SWITCH_HYSTERESIS,
        alt_min_separation_m: float = MPC_ALT_MIN_SEPARATION_M,
        alt_aperture_bias: float = MPC_ALT_APERTURE_BIAS,
        w_gap_clear: float = MPC_W_GAP_CLEAR,
        center_bias: float = MPC_CENTER_BIAS,
        track_width_cap_m: float = MPC_TRACK_WIDTH_CAP_M,
        corner_outer_bias: float = MPC_CORNER_OUTER_BIAS,
        inner_corner_margin_m: float = MPC_INNER_CORNER_MARGIN_M,
        blind_turn_kappa: float = MPC_BLIND_TURN_KAPPA,
        brake_decel_scale: float = MPC_BRAKE_DECEL_SCALE,
        narrow_corridor_v_gain: float = MPC_NARROW_CORRIDOR_V_GAIN,
        use_corridor_flow: int = MPC_USE_CORRIDOR_FLOW,
        fix_yaw_sign: int = 1,
        mpc_v_nom_min: float = 1.20,
        mpc_max_iter: int = MPC_MAX_ITER,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self._costmap = costmap_engine
        self.N = int(horizon_N)
        self.dt = float(dt)
        self.q_progress = float(q_progress)
        self.q_cte = float(q_cte)
        self.q_heading = float(q_heading)
        self.r_steer = float(r_steer)
        self.r_accel = float(r_accel)
        self.r_steer_rate = float(r_steer_rate)
        self.mu_friction = float(mu_friction)
        self.v_corner_factor = float(v_corner_factor)
        self.v_cruise_max = float(v_cruise_max)
        self.v_cruise_min = float(v_cruise_min)
        self.alt_switch_hysteresis = float(alt_switch_hysteresis)
        self.alt_min_separation_m = float(alt_min_separation_m)
        self.alt_aperture_bias = float(alt_aperture_bias)
        self.w_gap_clear = float(kwargs.get("alt_lookahead_depth_weight", w_gap_clear))
        self.center_bias = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(center_bias)))
        self.track_width_cap_m = max(0.0, float(track_width_cap_m))
        self.corner_outer_bias = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(corner_outer_bias)))
        self.inner_corner_margin_m = max(0.0, min(0.25, float(inner_corner_margin_m)))
        self.blind_turn_kappa = max(0.0, min(3.0, float(blind_turn_kappa)))
        self.brake_decel_scale = max(0.4, min(1.5, float(brake_decel_scale)))
        self.narrow_corridor_v_gain = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(narrow_corridor_v_gain)))
        self.use_corridor_flow = int(bool(use_corridor_flow))
        self.fix_yaw_sign = int(bool(fix_yaw_sign))
        self.mpc_v_nom_min = max(0.80, min(3.50, float(mpc_v_nom_min)))
        self.mpc_max_iter = max(6, min(35, int(mpc_max_iter)))
        self.dt_actuator = DT_PLANNER
        self.nx = 7
        self.nu = 2
        self._last_winner = 0
        self._active_target: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None
        self._last_alt_target: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None

        # Preallocated ctypes outputs
        self._out_steer = ctypes.c_double(0.0)
        self._out_speed = ctypes.c_double(0.0)
        self._out_best_cost = ctypes.c_double(0.0)
        self._out_osqp_iters = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._out_status_code = ctypes.c_int(0)

        self._max_pts = 64
        self._buf_xs1 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys1 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_xs2 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys2 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_xs3 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys3 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_xs4 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys4 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()

        self._buf_xs5 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys5 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_xs6 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()
        self._buf_ys6 = (ctypes.c_double * self._max_pts)()

        self._shield_k1_l_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k1_l_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k1_r_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k1_r_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)

        self._shield_k2_l_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k2_l_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k2_r_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_k2_r_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)

        self._shield_straight_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_straight_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_straight_zero_cnt = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_straight_zero_safe = ctypes.c_int(0)

        self._shield_active = ctypes.c_int(0)
        self._shield_chosen = ctypes.c_int(-1)

        cm_handle, probe_fn_ptr = self._resolve_costmap()

        # Handle 1: Primary Racing Line solver
        self._handle = _lib.sl_mpc_create(
            cm_handle,
            probe_fn_ptr,
            int(self.N),
            float(self.dt),
            float(self.q_progress),
            float(self.q_cte),
            float(self.q_heading),
            float(self.r_steer),
            float(self.r_accel),
            float(self.r_steer_rate),
            float(self.mu_friction),
            float(self.v_corner_factor),
            float(self.v_cruise_max),
            float(self.v_cruise_min),
        )

        # Handle 2: Smart Challenger Alternative Line solver
        self._handle_alt = _lib.sl_mpc_create(
            cm_handle,
            probe_fn_ptr,
            int(self.N),
            float(self.dt),
            float(self.q_progress),
            float(self.q_cte),
            float(self.q_heading),
            float(self.r_steer),
            float(self.r_accel),
            float(self.r_steer_rate),
            float(self.mu_friction),
            float(self.v_corner_factor),
            float(self.v_cruise_max),
            float(self.v_cruise_min),
        )

        if not self._handle:
            raise RuntimeError("Failed to create C++ CurvilinearSLMPCNative instance")

        # Deterministic 60 Hz iteration caps (prevents tail latency overruns on ARM Cortex-A72)
        if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_max_iter"):
            if self._handle:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_max_iter(self._handle, 14)
            if self._handle_alt:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_max_iter(self._handle_alt, 6)

        self.set_center_bias(self.center_bias)
        self.set_racing_params(
            track_width_cap_m=self.track_width_cap_m,
            corner_outer_bias=self.corner_outer_bias,
            inner_corner_margin_m=self.inner_corner_margin_m,
            blind_turn_kappa=self.blind_turn_kappa,
            brake_decel_scale=self.brake_decel_scale,
            narrow_corridor_v_gain=self.narrow_corridor_v_gain,
            use_corridor_flow=self.use_corridor_flow,
        )
        if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_advanced_params"):
            for h in (self._handle, self._handle_alt):
                if h:
                    _lib.sl_mpc_set_advanced_params(
                        h, ctypes.c_int(self.fix_yaw_sign), ctypes.c_double(self.mpc_v_nom_min)
                    )

    def reset_active_target(self) -> None:
        """Resets the currently tracked active corridor/target line and OSQP solver warm-start."""
        self._active_target = None
        self._last_alt_target = None
        self._last_winner = 0
        if _lib is not None and hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_reset"):
            if hasattr(self, "_handle") and self._handle:
                _lib.sl_mpc_reset(self._handle)
            if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
                _lib.sl_mpc_reset(self._handle_alt)

    def set_dynamic_mu(self, new_mu: float) -> None:
        """
        Dynamically updates the tire friction coefficient governing the grip envelope
        and OSQP solver constraints in the native C++ Curvilinear SL-MPC planner.
        """
        self.mu_friction = float(new_mu)
        if _lib is not None and hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_mu"):
            if hasattr(self, "_handle") and self._handle:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_mu(self._handle, ctypes.c_double(self.mu_friction))
            if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_mu(self._handle_alt, ctypes.c_double(self.mu_friction))

    def set_center_bias(self, center_bias: float) -> None:
        """Updates the corridor centering blend [0.0, 1.0] on both primary and challenger C++ solvers."""
        self.center_bias = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(center_bias)))
        if _lib is not None and hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_center_bias"):
            if hasattr(self, "_handle") and self._handle:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_center_bias(self._handle, ctypes.c_double(self.center_bias))
            if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_center_bias(self._handle_alt, ctypes.c_double(self.center_bias))

    def set_racing_params(
        self,
        track_width_cap_m: float = 0.0,
        corner_outer_bias: float = 0.0,
        inner_corner_margin_m: float = 0.0,
        blind_turn_kappa: float = 0.0,
        brake_decel_scale: float = 1.0,
        narrow_corridor_v_gain: float = 0.0,
        use_corridor_flow: int = 0,
    ) -> None:
        """Updates racing-line and corridor-flow parameters on both primary and challenger solvers."""
        self.track_width_cap_m = max(0.0, float(track_width_cap_m))
        self.corner_outer_bias = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(corner_outer_bias)))
        self.inner_corner_margin_m = max(0.0, min(0.25, float(inner_corner_margin_m)))
        self.blind_turn_kappa = max(0.0, min(3.0, float(blind_turn_kappa)))
        self.brake_decel_scale = max(0.4, min(1.5, float(brake_decel_scale)))
        self.narrow_corridor_v_gain = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(narrow_corridor_v_gain)))
        self.use_corridor_flow = int(bool(use_corridor_flow))
        if _lib is not None and hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_racing_params"):
            for h in (getattr(self, "_handle", None), getattr(self, "_handle_alt", None)):
                if h:
                    _lib.sl_mpc_set_racing_params(
                        h,
                        ctypes.c_double(self.track_width_cap_m),
                        ctypes.c_double(self.corner_outer_bias),
                        ctypes.c_double(self.inner_corner_margin_m),
                        ctypes.c_double(self.blind_turn_kappa),
                        ctypes.c_double(self.brake_decel_scale),
                        ctypes.c_double(self.narrow_corridor_v_gain),
                        ctypes.c_int(self.use_corridor_flow),
                    )

    @property
    def alt_lookahead_depth_weight(self) -> float:
        return self.w_gap_clear

    @alt_lookahead_depth_weight.setter
    def alt_lookahead_depth_weight(self, val: float):
        self.w_gap_clear = float(val)

    @property
    def costmap(self):
        return getattr(self, "_costmap", None)

    @costmap.setter
    def costmap(self, cm):
        self._costmap = cm
        cm_handle, probe_fn_ptr = self._resolve_costmap()
        if hasattr(self, "_handle") and self._handle:
            _lib.sl_mpc_set_costmap(self._handle, cm_handle, probe_fn_ptr)
        if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
            _lib.sl_mpc_set_costmap(self._handle_alt, cm_handle, probe_fn_ptr)

    def _resolve_costmap(self):
        """Extracts native costmap pointer and direct C-ABI probe function pointer."""
        if self._costmap is None:
            return None, None

        if hasattr(self._costmap, "_handle") and self._costmap._handle:
            try:
                import cpp_smart_hybrid_costmap
                lib_cm = cpp_smart_hybrid_costmap._lib
                probe_addr = ctypes.cast(lib_cm.costmap_probe_path, ctypes.c_void_p).value
                return ctypes.c_void_p(self._costmap._handle), ctypes.c_void_p(probe_addr)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Warning] SL-MPC native costmap probe unavailable ({e}); "
                      f"falling back to SLOW Python probe callback", flush=True)

        if hasattr(self._costmap, "probe_path"):
            def _py_probe_cb(h, xs_ptr, ys_ptr, count):
                xs_arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(xs_ptr, shape=(count,))
                ys_arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(ys_ptr, shape=(count,))
                return 1 if self._costmap.probe_path(xs_arr, ys_arr) else 0

            self._cb_ref = PROBE_CALLBACK_TYPE(_py_probe_cb)
            fn_addr = ctypes.cast(self._cb_ref, ctypes.c_void_p).value
            return None, ctypes.c_void_p(fn_addr)

        return None, None

    def plan(
        self,
        xg: float,
        yg: float,
        theta_g: float,
        aperture_width: float,
        v_current: float,
        steer_current: float,
        yaw_rate_current: float,
        extra_targets: Optional[List[Tuple[float, float, float]]] = None,
        x0: float = 0.0,
        y0: float = 0.0,
        psi0: float = 0.0,
        t_frame_start: Optional[float] = None,
        frame_budget_ms: float = 15.5,
        pitch_rad: float = 0.0,
        full_telemetry: bool = True,
    ) -> Dict[str, any]:
        """
        Solves the Curvilinear SL-MPC QP using high-speed native C++ core.
        Tracks active line identity, associates perception candidates to Incumbent vs Challenger,
        and arbitrates using alt_switch_hysteresis cost margin.
        """
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 0. CANDIDATE ASSOCIATION (INCUMBENT VS CHALLENGER)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        targets = []
        if extra_targets and len(extra_targets) > 0:
            targets = list(extra_targets)
        elif xg is not None and yg is not None:
            targets = [(float(xg), float(yg), float(aperture_width))]

        if not targets:
            targets = [(0.0, 1.20, 0.40)]

        prim_target = targets[0]
        alt_target = targets[1] if len(targets) >= 2 else None

        # Associate incoming stateless candidates with tracked active target
        if self._active_target is not None and len(targets) >= 2:
            act_x, act_y = self._active_target
            d0 = math.hypot(targets[0][0] - act_x, targets[0][1] - act_y)
            d1 = math.hypot(targets[1][0] - act_x, targets[1][1] - act_y)

            is_clear_0 = bool(targets[0][3]) if len(targets[0]) >= 4 else (math.hypot(targets[0][0], targets[0][1]) >= 2.20)
            is_clear_1 = bool(targets[1][3]) if len(targets[1]) >= 4 else (math.hypot(targets[1][0], targets[1][1]) >= 2.20)

            # If target 1 is closer to our currently followed line, target 1 is Incumbent
            # UNLESS target 1 is blocked by an obstacle while target 0 is completely clear (1.8m opening).
            if d1 < d0 and d1 <= 0.45:
                if (not is_clear_1) and is_clear_0:
                    prim_target = targets[0]
                    alt_target = targets[1]
                else:
                    prim_target = targets[1]
                    alt_target = targets[0]
            elif d0 <= 0.45:
                if (not is_clear_0) and is_clear_1:
                    prim_target = targets[1]
                    alt_target = targets[0]
                else:
                    prim_target = targets[0]
                    alt_target = targets[1]

        tx_prim, ty_prim, ap_prim = prim_target[:3]
        is_clear_prim = bool(prim_target[3]) if len(prim_target) >= 4 else (math.hypot(tx_prim, ty_prim) >= 2.20)
        theta_prim = math.atan2(tx_prim - x0, max(0.20, ty_prim - y0))

        has_opposite_gap = 0
        if alt_target is not None:
            dx_p = tx_prim - x0
            dx_a = alt_target[0] - x0
            is_clear_a = bool(alt_target[3]) if len(alt_target) >= 4 else (math.hypot(alt_target[0], alt_target[1]) >= 1.40)
            if (dx_p * dx_a < -0.005) and (is_clear_a or math.hypot(alt_target[0] - x0, alt_target[1] - y0) >= 1.10):
                has_opposite_gap = 1

        if _lib is not None and hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_context"):
            if self._handle:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_context(self._handle, ctypes.c_double(float(pitch_rad)), ctypes.c_int(has_opposite_gap))
            if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
                _lib.sl_mpc_set_context(self._handle_alt, ctypes.c_double(float(pitch_rad)), ctypes.c_int(has_opposite_gap))

        t_opt_0 = time.perf_counter()
        t_opt_cpu_0 = time.process_time()

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. SOLVE INCUMBENT (PRIMARY) RACING LINE
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Dynamic Primary OSQP Iteration Scaling based on whole-pipeline budget elapsed so far:
        # Prevents tail-latency overruns when perception (costmap + gap scout) took longer.
        if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_max_iter") and self._handle:
            if t_frame_start is not None:
                elapsed_prior_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_frame_start) * 1000.0
                target_deadline_ms = min(float(frame_budget_ms), 13.2)
                budget_for_prim_ms = target_deadline_ms - elapsed_prior_ms - 0.5  # Reserve 0.5ms for shield
                if budget_for_prim_ms >= 8.0:
                    max_iter_prim = self.mpc_max_iter
                elif budget_for_prim_ms >= 4.5:
                    max_iter_prim = min(10, self.mpc_max_iter)
                else:
                    max_iter_prim = 6
            else:
                max_iter_prim = self.mpc_max_iter
            _lib.sl_mpc_set_max_iter(self._handle, max_iter_prim)

        t_prim_0 = time.perf_counter()
        if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_plan_projected"):
            success_prim = _lib.sl_mpc_plan_projected(
                self._handle,
                float(tx_prim), float(ty_prim), float(theta_prim), float(ap_prim),
                float(v_current), float(steer_current), float(yaw_rate_current),
                float(x0), float(y0), float(psi0),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_steer),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_speed),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_best_cost),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_osqp_iters),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_status_code),
            )
        else:
            success_prim = _lib.sl_mpc_plan(
                self._handle,
                float(tx_prim), float(ty_prim), float(theta_prim), float(ap_prim),
                float(v_current), float(steer_current), float(yaw_rate_current),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_steer),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_speed),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_best_cost),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_osqp_iters),
                ctypes.byref(self._out_status_code),
            )

        status_prim = self._out_status_code.value
        cmd_steer_prim = float(self._out_steer.value)
        cmd_speed_prim = float(self._out_speed.value)
        cost_prim = float(self._out_best_cost.value)
        iters_prim = int(self._out_osqp_iters.value)

        if full_telemetry:
            n_best_prim = _lib.sl_mpc_get_best_line(self._handle, self._buf_xs1, self._buf_ys1, self._max_pts)
            best_line_prim = [[float(self._buf_xs1[i]), float(self._buf_ys1[i])] for i in range(n_best_prim)]
        else:
            best_line_prim = []
        guide_line_prim = []
        t_prim_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_prim_0) * 1000.0

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. SOLVE SMART CHALLENGER ALTERNATIVE LINE (if available)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        has_challenger = False
        status_alt = -1
        cmd_steer_alt = 0.0
        cmd_speed_alt = self.v_cruise_min
        cost_alt = 1e9
        best_line_alt = []
        t_alt_ms = 0.0
        iters_alt = 0

        # Budget-conscious challenger solve with at least 1 iteration:
        # When distinct alternative target exists (has_sep):
        # - Capped iteration budget (8 iters) if remaining frame budget >= 5.5 ms.
        # - Fallback to single iteration (max_iter = 1) when budget is tight (>= 3.2 ms).
        # - Hard skip challenger if remaining budget < 3.2 ms to guarantee strict 60 Hz compliance.
        if alt_target is not None and hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
            tx_alt, ty_alt, ap_alt = alt_target[:3]
            is_clear_alt = bool(alt_target[3]) if len(alt_target) >= 4 else (math.hypot(tx_alt, ty_alt) >= 2.20)
            min_sep = getattr(self, "alt_min_separation_m", 0.06)
            has_sep = (math.hypot(tx_alt - tx_prim, ty_alt - ty_prim) >= min_sep or abs(tx_alt - tx_prim) >= (min_sep * 0.7))

            if has_sep:
                # Strict Whole-Pipeline Frame Budget Enforcement (Guaranteed < 16.6 ms worst-case):
                # Measure total time spent across the ENTIRE single-frame pipeline so far:
                # (perception + motion latency comp + costmap update + gap scout + primary MPC solve)
                t_check = time.perf_counter()
                elapsed_so_far_ms = ((t_check - t_frame_start) * 1000.0) if t_frame_start is not None else t_prim_ms
                target_deadline_ms = min(float(frame_budget_ms), 13.2)
                budget_remaining_ms = target_deadline_ms - elapsed_so_far_ms

                # Hard budget allocation (calibrated to ARM64 Cortex-A72 execution profile):
                # - High budget (10 iters): ONLY IF budget_remaining >= 6.5 ms
                # - Single iter (1 iter, ~1.2 ms + 0.5 ms shield): IF budget_remaining >= 4.8 ms
                # - Under 4.8 ms: HARD SKIP challenger to guarantee worst-case frame strictly < 16.6 ms!
                if budget_remaining_ms >= 6.5:
                    has_challenger = True
                    max_iter_alt = 10
                elif budget_remaining_ms >= 4.8:
                    has_challenger = True
                    max_iter_alt = 1
                else:
                    has_challenger = False

                if has_challenger:
                    if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_reset"):
                        if (
                            self._last_alt_target is None
                            or math.hypot(tx_alt - self._last_alt_target[0], ty_alt - self._last_alt_target[1]) > 0.45
                        ):
                            _lib.sl_mpc_reset(self._handle_alt)
                    self._last_alt_target = (float(tx_alt), float(ty_alt))
                    if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_set_max_iter"):
                        _lib.sl_mpc_set_max_iter(self._handle_alt, max_iter_alt)
                    t_alt_0 = time.perf_counter()
                    theta_alt = math.atan2(tx_alt - x0, max(0.20, ty_alt - y0))
                    if hasattr(_lib, "sl_mpc_plan_projected"):
                        _lib.sl_mpc_plan_projected(
                            self._handle_alt,
                            float(tx_alt), float(ty_alt), float(theta_alt), float(ap_alt),
                            float(v_current), float(steer_current), float(yaw_rate_current),
                            float(x0), float(y0), float(psi0),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_steer),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_speed),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_best_cost),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_osqp_iters),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_status_code),
                        )
                    else:
                        _lib.sl_mpc_plan(
                            self._handle_alt,
                            float(tx_alt), float(ty_alt), float(theta_alt), float(ap_alt),
                            float(v_current), float(steer_current), float(yaw_rate_current),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_steer),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_speed),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_best_cost),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_osqp_iters),
                            ctypes.byref(self._out_status_code),
                        )
                    status_alt = self._out_status_code.value
                    iters_alt = int(self._out_osqp_iters.value)
                    cmd_steer_alt = float(self._out_steer.value)
                    cmd_speed_alt = float(self._out_speed.value)
                    cost_alt = float(self._out_best_cost.value)
                    if full_telemetry:
                        n_best_alt = _lib.sl_mpc_get_best_line(self._handle_alt, self._buf_xs1, self._buf_ys1, self._max_pts)
                        best_line_alt = [[float(self._buf_xs1[i]), float(self._buf_ys1[i])] for i in range(n_best_alt)]
                    t_alt_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_alt_0) * 1000.0

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. ARBITRATION BETWEEN INCUMBENT AND CHALLENGER
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        candidate_lines = (
            ([best_line_prim, best_line_alt] if (has_challenger and best_line_alt) else [best_line_prim])
            if full_telemetry else []
        )

        winner = 0
        depth_credit = 0.0
        if has_challenger:
            primary_ok = (status_prim == 0)
            alt_ok = (status_alt == 0)
            if not primary_ok and alt_ok:
                # Primary line blocked/infeasible, challenger clear: immediate safety switch
                winner = 1
            elif primary_ok and not alt_ok:
                # Challenger infeasible, primary safe: retain primary
                winner = 0
            elif is_clear_alt and not is_clear_prim:
                # Challenger reaches unobstructed open horizon while primary terminates in an obstacle:
                # The clear opening decisively takes precedence over the obstructed path!
                winner = 1
            elif is_clear_prim and not is_clear_alt:
                # Primary is unobstructed while challenger hits an obstacle:
                # Retain primary unobstructed racing line!
                winner = 0
            elif primary_ok and alt_ok:
                # Both are clear or both are obstructed: arbitrate using MPC dynamic cost with hysteresis and depth credit
                d_prim = math.hypot(tx_prim, ty_prim)
                d_alt = math.hypot(tx_alt, ty_alt)
                depth_diff = d_alt - d_prim

                # Base depth credit proportional to lookahead advantage
                depth_credit = self.w_gap_clear * depth_diff

                # Challenger must beat Incumbent by hysteresis cost margin minus lookahead depth credit
                if (cost_alt - depth_credit + self.alt_switch_hysteresis) < cost_prim:
                    winner = 1
                else:
                    winner = 0

        # Update active tracked target to the winning line's target
        if winner == 1 and alt_target is not None:
            self._active_target = (alt_target[0], alt_target[1])
        else:
            self._active_target = (prim_target[0], prim_target[1])
        self._last_winner = winner

        win_handle = self._handle_alt if (winner == 1 and hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt) else self._handle

        if full_telemetry:
            n_guide = _lib.sl_mpc_get_guide_line(win_handle, self._buf_xs1, self._buf_ys1, self._max_pts)
            guide_line_win = [[float(self._buf_xs1[i]), float(self._buf_ys1[i])] for i in range(n_guide)] if n_guide > 0 else guide_line_prim

            n_corr = _lib.sl_mpc_get_corridor(win_handle, self._buf_xs1, self._buf_ys1, self._buf_xs2, self._buf_ys2, self._max_pts)
            corridor_left = [[float(self._buf_xs1[i]), float(self._buf_ys1[i])] for i in range(n_corr)]
            corridor_right = [[float(self._buf_xs2[i]), float(self._buf_ys2[i])] for i in range(n_corr)]

            # Extract Emergency Shield evasion branches (Step 0 Left/Right, Step 2 Left/Right, MPC Straight, Zero-Steer Straight)
            t_shield_0 = time.perf_counter()
            _lib.sl_mpc_get_shield_branches(
                win_handle,
                self._buf_xs1, self._buf_ys1, ctypes.byref(self._shield_k1_l_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_k1_l_safe),
                self._buf_xs2, self._buf_ys2, ctypes.byref(self._shield_k1_r_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_k1_r_safe),
                self._buf_xs3, self._buf_ys3, ctypes.byref(self._shield_k2_l_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_k2_l_safe),
                self._buf_xs4, self._buf_ys4, ctypes.byref(self._shield_k2_r_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_k2_r_safe),
                self._buf_xs5, self._buf_ys5, ctypes.byref(self._shield_straight_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_straight_safe),
                self._buf_xs6, self._buf_ys6, ctypes.byref(self._shield_straight_zero_cnt), ctypes.byref(self._shield_straight_zero_safe),
                ctypes.byref(self._shield_active), ctypes.byref(self._shield_chosen)
            )

            n_k1_l = int(self._shield_k1_l_cnt.value)
            n_k1_r = int(self._shield_k1_r_cnt.value)
            n_k2_l = int(self._shield_k2_l_cnt.value)
            n_k2_r = int(self._shield_k2_r_cnt.value)
            n_str  = int(self._shield_straight_cnt.value)
            n_str_zero = int(self._shield_straight_zero_cnt.value)

            k1_left_branch  = [[float(self._buf_xs1[i]), float(self._buf_ys1[i])] for i in range(n_k1_l)] if n_k1_l > 0 else []
            k1_right_branch = [[float(self._buf_xs2[i]), float(self._buf_ys2[i])] for i in range(n_k1_r)] if n_k1_r > 0 else []
            k2_left_branch  = [[float(self._buf_xs3[i]), float(self._buf_ys3[i])] for i in range(n_k2_l)] if n_k2_l > 0 else []
            k2_right_branch = [[float(self._buf_xs4[i]), float(self._buf_ys4[i])] for i in range(n_k2_r)] if n_k2_r > 0 else []
            straight_branch = [[float(self._buf_xs5[i]), float(self._buf_ys5[i])] for i in range(n_str)]  if n_str  > 0 else []
            straight_zero_branch = [[float(self._buf_xs6[i]), float(self._buf_ys6[i])] for i in range(n_str_zero)] if n_str_zero > 0 else []
        else:
            guide_line_win = []
            corridor_left = []
            corridor_right = []
            t_shield_0 = time.perf_counter()
            _lib.sl_mpc_get_shield_branches(
                win_handle,
                None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None,
                None, None, None, None,
                ctypes.byref(self._shield_active), ctypes.byref(self._shield_chosen)
            )

        chosen_id = int(self._shield_chosen.value)
        chosen_name = None
        if chosen_id == 0:
            chosen_name = "k2_left"
        elif chosen_id == 1:
            chosen_name = "k2_right"
        elif chosen_id == 2:
            chosen_name = "k1_left"
        elif chosen_id == 3:
            chosen_name = "k1_right"
        elif chosen_id == 4:
            chosen_name = "reverse"  # All evasion paths blocked — recovery manager should reverse

        if full_telemetry:
            shield_info = {
                "active": bool(self._shield_active.value),
                "chosen_id": chosen_id,
                "chosen_name": chosen_name,
                "reverse_suggested": (chosen_id == 4),  # True when all 5-step evasion arcs are blocked
                "k1_left": k1_left_branch,
                "k1_left_safe": bool(self._shield_k1_l_safe.value),
                "k1_right": k1_right_branch,
                "k1_right_safe": bool(self._shield_k1_r_safe.value),
                "k2_left": k2_left_branch,
                "k2_left_safe": bool(self._shield_k2_l_safe.value),
                "k2_right": k2_right_branch,
                "k2_right_safe": bool(self._shield_k2_r_safe.value),
                "straight": straight_branch,
                "straight_safe": bool(self._shield_straight_safe.value),
                "straight_mpc": straight_branch,
                "straight_mpc_safe": bool(self._shield_straight_safe.value),
                "straight_zero": straight_zero_branch,
                "straight_zero_safe": bool(self._shield_straight_zero_safe.value),
            }
        else:
            shield_info = {
                "active": bool(self._shield_active.value),
                "chosen_id": chosen_id,
                "chosen_name": chosen_name,
                "reverse_suggested": (chosen_id == 4),
            }
        t_shield_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_shield_0) * 1000.0

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. PACKAGE ACTIVE CONTROL COMMANDS
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        shield_is_active = bool(self._shield_active.value)
        if winner == 1:
            best_line = best_line_alt
            cmd_steer = cmd_steer_alt
            cmd_speed = cmd_speed_alt
            status_desc = "CHALLENGER_OVERTHROW"
            active_cost = cost_alt
        elif status_prim == 0:
            best_line = best_line_prim
            cmd_steer = cmd_steer_prim
            cmd_speed = cmd_speed_prim
            status_desc = "OPTIMAL_OSQP_MPC"
            active_cost = cost_prim
        else:
            best_line = best_line_prim
            cmd_steer = cmd_steer_prim
            cmd_speed = cmd_speed_prim if shield_is_active else self.v_cruise_min
            status_desc = "FALLBACK_SL_MPC"
            active_cost = cost_prim

        t_opt_total_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_opt_0) * 1000.0
        t_opt_cpu_ms = (time.process_time() - t_opt_cpu_0) * 1000.0

        return {
            "cmd_steer_rad": cmd_steer,
            "cmd_speed_mps": cmd_speed,
            "osqp_status": "solved" if (status_prim == 0 or status_alt == 0) else "fallback",
            "osqp_iters": iters_prim,
            "osqp_iters_alt": iters_alt,
            "status_prim": status_prim,
            "status_alt": status_alt if has_challenger else None,
            "best_cost": active_cost,
            "cost_primary": cost_prim,
            "cost_alt": cost_alt if has_challenger else None,
            "depth_credit": depth_credit,
            "winner_id": winner,
            "status": status_desc,
            "best_line": best_line,
            "guide_line": guide_line_win,
            "candidate_lines": candidate_lines,
            "corridor_left": corridor_left,
            "corridor_right": corridor_right,
            "shield": shield_info,
            "opt_ms": round(t_opt_total_ms, 2),
            "opt_cpu_ms": round(t_opt_cpu_ms, 2),
            "opt_prim_ms": round(t_prim_ms, 2),
            "opt_alt_ms": round(t_alt_ms, 2),
            "opt_shield_ms": round(t_shield_ms, 2),
        }

    def __del__(self):
        if _lib is not None:
            if hasattr(self, "_handle") and self._handle:
                try:
                    _lib.sl_mpc_destroy(self._handle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._handle = None
            if hasattr(self, "_handle_alt") and self._handle_alt:
                try:
                    _lib.sl_mpc_destroy(self._handle_alt)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._handle_alt = None


# Primary export alias
CurvilinearSLMPC = CppCurvilinearSLMPC

__all__ = [
    "CurvilinearSLMPC",
    "CppCurvilinearSLMPC",
    "CPP_AVAILABLE",
]


=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/progress_tracker.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  WEBOTS TRACK PROGRESS TRACKER & WALL GEOMETRY LOADER
================================================================================
  Unified module for computing true ground-truth distance along the track
  centerline without shortcuts, wall-bounces, or reverse progress.
================================================================================
"""

import os
import re
import math
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np

# Resolution of directory paths relative to this file
CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CONTROLLERS_DIR = os.path.dirname(CURR_DIR)
MY_PROJECT_DIR = os.path.dirname(CONTROLLERS_DIR)


def load_track_walls(world_path: Optional[str] = None) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Loads exact left and right wall point coordinates from track_racing.wbt."""
    if world_path is None:
        world_path = os.path.join(MY_PROJECT_DIR, "worlds", "track_racing.wbt")

    if not os.path.exists(world_path):
        return np.zeros((0, 2)), np.zeros((0, 2))

    try:
        with open(world_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            text = f.read()

        left_pos = text.find("track_left_wall")
        right_pos = text.find("track_right_wall")

        def extract_pts(start_idx: int) -> np.ndarray:
            if start_idx == -1:
                return np.zeros((0, 2))
            m = re.search(r"Coordinate\s*\{\s*point\s*\[\s*([-\d\.\s,]+)\]", text[start_idx:])
            if m:
                return np.array([float(x) for x in m.group(1).replace(",", " ").split() if x]).reshape(-1, 3)[:, :2]
            return np.zeros((0, 2))

        left_pts = extract_pts(left_pos)
        right_pts = extract_pts(right_pos)
        return left_pts, right_pts
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[ProgressTracker] Warning: Could not load track walls from {world_path}: {e}", flush=True)

    return np.zeros((0, 2)), np.zeros((0, 2))


class WebotsTrackProgressTracker:
    """
    Monotonically tracks true ground-truth progress along the racing line.
    Prevents cheating from:
      - Driving back and forth (distance only advances when pushing new frontiers)
      - Wiggling side to side (orthogonal projection keeps arc-length invariant)
      - Wall bouncing / shuddering
      - Reverse driving
    """

    def __init__(self, traj_csv_path: str):
        self.traj_pts = np.zeros((0, 2))
        self.dists = np.zeros(0)
        self.total_length = 14.109
        self.n_pts = 0

        if os.path.exists(traj_csv_path):
            try:
                arr = np.loadtxt(traj_csv_path, delimiter=",", skiprows=1)
                self.traj_pts = arr[:, 0:2]
                if arr.shape[1] >= 6:
                    self.dists = arr[:, 5]
                else:
                    diffs = np.diff(self.traj_pts, axis=0)
                    seg_dists = np.hypot(diffs[:, 0], diffs[:, 1])
                    self.dists = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(seg_dists)])

                if len(self.traj_pts) > 1:
                    closing_gap = float(np.hypot(self.traj_pts[0, 0] - self.traj_pts[-1, 0], self.traj_pts[0, 1] - self.traj_pts[-1, 1]))
                    self.total_length = float(self.dists[-1] + closing_gap)
                else:
                    self.total_length = float(self.dists[-1])
                self.n_pts = len(self.dists)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ProgressTracker] Warning: Failed to load trajectory {traj_csv_path}: {e}", flush=True)

        self.last_idx = 0
        self.prev_s = 0.0
        self.current_lap = 0
        self.current_track_dist = 0.0
        self.max_track_progress = 0.0
        self.prev_pos = np.array([0.0, 0.0])
        self.off_track = False

    def _project_point(self, p: np.ndarray, i0: int, i1: int) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        A = self.traj_pts[i0]
        B = self.traj_pts[i1]
        v = B - A
        v_sq = float(np.dot(v, v))
        if v_sq < 1e-9:
            return 0.0, float(np.linalg.norm(p - A)), 0.0
        t = float(np.clip(np.dot(p - A, v) / v_sq, 0.0, 1.0))
        proj = A + t * v
        d_perp = float(np.linalg.norm(p - proj))
        seg_len = math.sqrt(v_sq)
        return t, d_perp, seg_len

    def reset(self, start_pos: List[float]) -> None:
        if self.n_pts < 2:
            return
        cx, cy = float(start_pos[0]), float(start_pos[1])
        dists_sq = (self.traj_pts[:, 0] - cx) ** 2 + (self.traj_pts[:, 1] - cy) ** 2
        self.last_idx = int(np.argmin(dists_sq))

        p = np.array([cx, cy])
        i_next = (self.last_idx + 1) % self.n_pts
        t, _, seg_len = self._project_point(p, self.last_idx, i_next)
        self.prev_s = float(self.dists[self.last_idx] + t * seg_len)
        self.current_lap = 0
        self.current_track_dist = 0.0
        self.max_track_progress = 0.0
        self.prev_pos = np.array([cx, cy], dtype=float)
        self.off_track = False

    def update(self, current_pos: List[float]) -> float:
        if self.n_pts < 2:
            return 0.0
        cx, cy = float(current_pos[0]), float(current_pos[1])
        p = np.array([cx, cy])

        # Local search window: -15 to +35 waypoints around last tracked index
        search_window = [(self.last_idx + offset) % self.n_pts for offset in range(-15, 35)]
        cand_pts = self.traj_pts[search_window]
        dists_sq = (cand_pts[:, 0] - cx) ** 2 + (cand_pts[:, 1] - cy) ** 2
        best_local_idx = int(np.argmin(dists_sq))
        closest_idx = search_window[best_local_idx]

        # Test segments before and after closest waypoint
        i_prev = (closest_idx - 1) % self.n_pts
        i_next = (closest_idx + 1) % self.n_pts

        t_bwd, d_bwd, seg_bwd = self._project_point(p, i_prev, closest_idx)
        t_fwd, d_fwd, seg_fwd = self._project_point(p, closest_idx, i_next)

        if d_fwd <= d_bwd:
            seg_start = closest_idx
            t_chosen = t_fwd
            seg_len = seg_fwd
            min_d_perp = d_fwd
        else:
            seg_start = i_prev
            t_chosen = t_bwd
            seg_len = seg_bwd
            min_d_perp = d_bwd

        # Off track check (track width is 1.0m; racing line hugs inner apex so outer wall is up to 0.86m away)
        if min_d_perp > 0.95:
            self.off_track = True
            return self.max_track_progress

        s_curr = float(self.dists[seg_start] + t_chosen * seg_len)
        ds = s_curr - self.prev_s

        if ds < -0.5 * self.total_length:
            ds += self.total_length
        elif ds > 0.5 * self.total_length:
            ds -= self.total_length

        # Physical ceiling guard: clamp step jump at 0.25m
        step_euclid = float(np.hypot(cx - self.prev_pos[0], cy - self.prev_pos[1]))
        if ds > 0.25:
            ds = min(step_euclid, 0.25)

        self.current_track_dist += ds
        self.current_track_dist = max(0.0, self.current_track_dist)

        # Monotonic track progression: only advances when new forward ground is broken
        if self.current_track_dist > self.max_track_progress:
            self.max_track_progress = self.current_track_dist

        self.last_idx = closest_idx
        self.prev_s = s_curr
        self.prev_pos = np.array([cx, cy])
        self.current_lap = int(self.max_track_progress // self.total_length)
        return self.max_track_progress

    @property
    def total_progress_m(self) -> float:
        return self.max_track_progress


=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/ramp_detector.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  RAMP DETECTION & SPEED GOVERNOR MODULE
================================================================================
  Detects when the vehicle is ascending, traversing, or descending a ramp /
  elevation incline based on IMU pitch and tilt angles.
  When a ramp is detected, enforces a safe cruising speed limit (e.g. 2.0 m/s)
  with hysteresis and an apex transition hold latch.
================================================================================
"""

import math
from typing import Dict, Any

try:
    from .planner_config import (
        ENABLE_RAMP_DETECTION,
        RAMP_SPEED_LIMIT_MPS,
        RAMP_ENTER_PITCH_DEG,
        RAMP_ENTER_TILT_DEG,
        RAMP_EXIT_PITCH_DEG,
        RAMP_EXIT_TILT_DEG,
        RAMP_HOLD_TIME_S,
    )
except ImportError:
    from planner_config import (
        ENABLE_RAMP_DETECTION,
        RAMP_SPEED_LIMIT_MPS,
        RAMP_ENTER_PITCH_DEG,
        RAMP_ENTER_TILT_DEG,
        RAMP_EXIT_PITCH_DEG,
        RAMP_EXIT_TILT_DEG,
        RAMP_HOLD_TIME_S,
    )


class RampDetector:
    """
    Ramp detection and speed governance based on filtered IMU attitude.
    Replaces rollover detection to prevent unwanted countersteering and power cut
    on ramps, instead capping speed to a safe traversing limit.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        enabled: bool = ENABLE_RAMP_DETECTION,
        speed_limit_mps: float = RAMP_SPEED_LIMIT_MPS,
        enter_pitch_deg: float = RAMP_ENTER_PITCH_DEG,
        enter_tilt_deg: float = RAMP_ENTER_TILT_DEG,
        exit_pitch_deg: float = RAMP_EXIT_PITCH_DEG,
        exit_tilt_deg: float = RAMP_EXIT_TILT_DEG,
        hold_time_s: float = RAMP_HOLD_TIME_S,
        filter_alpha: float = 0.25,
    ):
        self.enabled = enabled
        self.speed_limit_mps = speed_limit_mps
        self.enter_pitch_deg = enter_pitch_deg
        self.enter_tilt_deg = enter_tilt_deg
        self.exit_pitch_deg = exit_pitch_deg
        self.exit_tilt_deg = exit_tilt_deg
        self.hold_time_s = hold_time_s
        self.filter_alpha = filter_alpha

        # Internal state
        self.is_on_ramp: bool = False
        self.hold_timer: float = 0.0
        self.filtered_pitch: float = 0.0
        self.filtered_roll: float = 0.0
        self._initialized: bool = False

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Resets detector state and timers."""
        self.is_on_ramp = False
        self.hold_timer = 0.0
        self.filtered_pitch = 0.0
        self.filtered_roll = 0.0
        self._initialized = False

    def update(
        self,
        pitch_rad: float,
        roll_rad: float,
        dt: float = 0.008,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Evaluates current attitude for ramp traversal.

        Args:
            pitch_rad: Vehicle pitch angle in radians (+ = nose up, - = nose down)
            roll_rad: Vehicle roll angle in radians (+ = lean right, - = lean left)
            dt: Timestep duration (seconds)

        Returns:
            Dictionary with ramp detection state, speed limit, and attitude angles.
        """
        if not self._initialized:
            self.filtered_pitch = pitch_rad
            self.filtered_roll = roll_rad
            self._initialized = True
        else:
            # Low-pass filter to reject high-frequency suspension vibration / bumps
            a = self.filter_alpha
            self.filtered_pitch = a * pitch_rad + (1.0 - a) * self.filtered_pitch
            self.filtered_roll = a * roll_rad + (1.0 - a) * self.filtered_roll

        pitch_deg = abs(math.degrees(self.filtered_pitch))
        roll_deg = abs(math.degrees(self.filtered_roll))
        tilt_deg = math.degrees(math.hypot(self.filtered_pitch, self.filtered_roll))

        if not self.enabled:
            return {
                "is_on_ramp": False,
                "speed_limit": self.speed_limit_mps,
                "pitch_deg": round(pitch_deg, 2),
                "roll_deg": round(roll_deg, 2),
                "tilt_deg": round(tilt_deg, 2),
                "hold_timer": 0.0,
            }

        # Check entry trigger: pitch or tilt exceeds entry threshold
        is_incline = (pitch_deg >= self.enter_pitch_deg) or (tilt_deg >= self.enter_tilt_deg)

        if is_incline:
            self.is_on_ramp = True
            self.hold_timer = self.hold_time_s
        elif self.hold_timer > 0.0:
            # Latch hold: maintain ramp mode while transitioning across flat apex or leaving ramp
            self.hold_timer = max(0.0, self.hold_timer - dt)
            if self.hold_timer <= 0.0:
                # Check exit hysteresis
                if (pitch_deg < self.exit_pitch_deg) and (tilt_deg < self.exit_tilt_deg):
                    self.is_on_ramp = False
                else:
                    # Still above exit hysteresis floor, re-arm hold timer
                    self.hold_timer = self.hold_time_s
        else:
            # Check exit threshold
            if (pitch_deg < self.exit_pitch_deg) and (tilt_deg < self.exit_tilt_deg):
                self.is_on_ramp = False

        return {
            "is_on_ramp": self.is_on_ramp,
            "speed_limit": self.speed_limit_mps,
            "pitch_deg": round(pitch_deg, 2),
            "roll_deg": round(roll_deg, 2),
            "tilt_deg": round(tilt_deg, 2),
            "hold_timer": round(self.hold_timer, 3),
        }


# Backwards compatibility alias
AntiRolloverController = RampDetector

=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/test_anti_wrong_way_options.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  OFFLINE TEST SUITE: ANTI-WRONG-WAY & U-TURN DISCRIMINATION WATCHDOG
================================================================================
  Validates all 4 options and hybrid mode against:
    1. Legitimate Track Hairpins (U-turn into adjacent corridor) -> Must NOT trigger
    2. In-Corridor Wrong-Way Reversals (spinning 180 inside same lane) -> MUST trigger
    3. Performance & compute latency (< 0.25 ms per cycle)
================================================================================
"""

import math
import sys
import time
import os

# Ensure local planners directory is in python path
CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if CURR_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, CURR_DIR)

import numpy as np

from anti_wrong_way_watchdog import (
    AntiWrongWayWatchdog,
    BreadcrumbWatchdog,
    Breadcrumb,
    WrongWayOptionMode,
    wrap_to_pi,
    AWW_BREADCRUMB_DURATION_SCALE,
    AWW_BREADCRUMB_MAX_TIMEOUT_S,
)
from planner_config import GRID_X_MIN, GRID_Y_MIN, GRID_RES
from stuck_recovery_manager import StuckRecoveryManager, StuckRecoveryState


def test_option1_breadcrumb():
    print("\n--- Testing Option 1: LOS Breadcrumb Raycasting & Anti-Contamination ---")
    dog = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, spacing_m=0.05, corridor_half_width_m=0.25)

    # 1. Drive forward along +Y axis from (0, 0) to (0, 3.0) with yaw = 0.0
    t = 0.0
    for i in range(60):
        x = 0.0
        y = i * 0.05
        yaw = 0.0
        v = 1.5
        dog.add_point(x, y, yaw, v, t, state="DRIVE", yaw_rate=0.0)
        t += 0.033

    # Anti-Contamination Verification: Points during spin / reverse must be dropped
    n_before = len(dog.breadcrumbs)
    dog.add_point(0.0, 3.05, 0.0, -0.5, t, state="PIVOT_REVERSE", yaw_rate=0.0)
    dog.add_point(0.0, 3.05, 0.0, 1.5, t, state="CORRECTIVE_180", yaw_rate=4.0)
    assert len(dog.breadcrumbs) == n_before, "Anti-contamination failed: points added during reverse/spin!"
    print(f"  [Anti-Contamination] Passed: buffer clean ({len(dog.breadcrumbs)} valid forward points)")

    # Test 2: Unobstructed 180 reversal in open track -> Raycast clear -> Wrong-way confirmed
    # Vehicle spins 180 in place at (0.0, 3.0) facing yaw = pi (looking back down +Y towards y=0)
    grid_h = int(round((2.60 - GRID_Y_MIN) / GRID_RES)) + 1
    grid_w = int(round((1.25 - GRID_X_MIN) / GRID_RES)) + 1
    hazard_mask_clear = np.zeros((grid_h, grid_w), dtype=np.uint8)
    is_ww, conf, target_yaw, rays = dog.check_wrong_way(0.0, 3.0, math.pi, hazard_mask=hazard_mask_clear)
    print(f"  [Test 2 - Open Track Reversal] Wrong Way: {is_ww} (Confidence: {conf:.2f}, Target Yaw: {target_yaw:.2f} rad, Rays: {len(rays)})")
    assert is_ww is True, "Option 1 failed to catch unobstructed in-lane wrong-way reversal!"
    assert conf >= 0.70, f"Expected confidence >= 0.70, got {conf}"
    assert abs(wrap_to_pi(target_yaw - 0.0)) < 0.1, f"Expected recovery target yaw ~ 0.0, got {target_yaw}"

    # Test 1: Hairpin track with wall in between -> Raycast blocked -> Zero false positives
    # Vehicle traversed 180 hairpin into adjacent corridor at x = 0.60 m, y = 2.0 m, facing yaw = pi
    # Dividing wall is at x_world = 0.30 m. In vehicle frame (at x=0.60, yaw=pi):
    # xv_wall = 0.30 m -> col_w = floor((0.30 - GRID_X_MIN) / GRID_RES)
    hazard_mask_wall = np.zeros((grid_h, grid_w), dtype=np.uint8)
    col_w = int(math.floor((0.30 - GRID_X_MIN) / GRID_RES))
    hazard_mask_wall[:, max(0, col_w - 3):min(grid_w, col_w + 4)] = 1  # ~10cm thick dividing wall between lanes

    is_ww_hairpin, conf_hp, _, rays_hp = dog.check_wrong_way(0.60, 2.0, math.pi, hazard_mask=hazard_mask_wall)
    print(f"  [Test 1 - Hairpin Dividing Wall] Wrong Way: {is_ww_hairpin} (Confidence: {conf_hp:.2f}, Blocked Rays: {sum(1 for r in rays_hp if not r['clear'])})")
    assert is_ww_hairpin is False, "Option 1 falsely triggered on hairpin with dividing wall!"
    assert conf_hp == 0.0, f"Expected confidence 0.0 on blocked sightlines, got {conf_hp}"

    print("  -> Option 1: PASSED (100% detection, 0% false positives)")


def test_smart_reverse_recovery():
    print("\n--- Testing Smart Reverse Recovery (Kinematics & Arbitration) ---")
    rec = StuckRecoveryManager(steer_angle_rad=0.60, reverse_speed_m_s=1.20)

    # Case 3A: Tightly Pinned Wall Escape (front ToF <= 0.03m / 3cm)
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.stroke_timer = 0.1

    # Left front is tightly pinned (2.5cm)
    tofs_left_pinned = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.025,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.50,
    }
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0, cached_tofs=tofs_left_pinned, current_yaw=0.0
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A - Left Pinned <= 3cm] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: > 0 steer LEFT to swing nose RIGHT)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd > 0.0, f"Expected positive steer (LEFT) when left-pinned, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Right front is tightly pinned (2.0cm)
    tofs_right_pinned = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.50,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.020,
    }
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0, cached_tofs=tofs_right_pinned, current_yaw=0.0
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A - Right Pinned <= 3cm] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: < 0 steer RIGHT to swing nose LEFT)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd < 0.0, f"Expected negative steer (RIGHT) when right-pinned, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Case 3A-Grid: Sparse Grid Cell Pinned Wall Escape (Inflated costmap front-corner checks)
    class DummyCostmap:
        res = 0.010
        x_min = -1.25
        y_min = 0.00

    dummy_cm = DummyCostmap()
    h, w = 190, 250
    tofs_nominal = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.50, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.50}

    # Left pinned grid test: mark left C-space corner (-0.035, 0.10) in hazard_mask
    mask_l_pinned = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)
    col_l = int(round((-0.035 - dummy_cm.x_min) / dummy_cm.res))
    row_l = int(round((0.10 - dummy_cm.y_min) / dummy_cm.res))
    mask_l_pinned[row_l, col_l] = 1

    is_l, is_r = rec.evaluate_pinned_state(mask_l_pinned, dummy_cm, tofs_nominal)
    assert is_l is True and is_r is False, f"Expected is_left_pinned=True, got l={is_l}, r={is_r}"

    # Breadcrumb wants to turn right in reverse (target_breadcrumb_yaw = +0.5 rad -> heading_err > 0 -> steer RIGHT)
    # But LEFT is pinned: steering right in reverse swings front-left nose into the left wall.
    # Therefore, left-pinned overrides and forces steer LEFT (> 0) to swing nose away from left wall!
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.stroke_timer = 0.1
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0,
        hazard_mask=mask_l_pinned, costmap_obj=dummy_cm,
        cached_tofs=tofs_nominal, current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=+0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A-Grid - Left Pinned Override] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: > 0 steer LEFT to swing nose away from left wall)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd > 0.0, f"Expected positive steer (LEFT) when left-pinned via grid, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Right pinned grid test: mark right C-space corner (+0.035, 0.10) in hazard_mask
    mask_r_pinned = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.uint8)
    col_r = int(round((+0.035 - dummy_cm.x_min) / dummy_cm.res))
    row_r = int(round((0.10 - dummy_cm.y_min) / dummy_cm.res))
    mask_r_pinned[row_r, col_r] = 1

    is_l, is_r = rec.evaluate_pinned_state(mask_r_pinned, dummy_cm, tofs_nominal)
    assert is_r is True and is_l is False, f"Expected is_right_pinned=True, got l={is_l}, r={is_r}"

    # Breadcrumb wants to turn left in reverse (target_breadcrumb_yaw = -0.5 rad -> heading_err < 0 -> steer LEFT)
    # But RIGHT is pinned: steering left in reverse swings front-right nose into the right wall.
    # Therefore, right-pinned overrides and forces steer RIGHT (< 0) to swing nose away from right wall!
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0,
        hazard_mask=mask_r_pinned, costmap_obj=dummy_cm,
        cached_tofs=tofs_nominal, current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=-0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A-Grid - Right Pinned Override] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: < 0 steer RIGHT to swing nose away from right wall)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd < 0.0, f"Expected negative steer (RIGHT) when right-pinned via grid, got {steer_cmd}"

    # When RIGHT is pinned, but breadcrumb wants to steer RIGHT (+0.5 rad -> steer RIGHT):
    # When RIGHT is pinned, but breadcrumb wants to steer RIGHT (+0.5 rad -> steer RIGHT):
    # Front nose swings LEFT (away from the right wall), so right obstacle does NOT pin it!
    # Car safely executes RIGHT steer lock (< 0) towards the breadcrumb!
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0,
        hazard_mask=mask_r_pinned, costmap_obj=dummy_cm,
        cached_tofs=tofs_nominal, current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=+0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A-Grid - Right Obstacle Non-Interfering] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: < 0 steer RIGHT towards breadcrumb)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd < 0.0, f"Expected negative steer (RIGHT) towards breadcrumb when right obstacle does not pin swing, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Both pinned test: both left and right obstacles present
    mask_both_pinned = mask_l_pinned | mask_r_pinned
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-1.0, commanded_v=-1.0,
        hazard_mask=mask_both_pinned, costmap_obj=dummy_cm,
        cached_tofs=tofs_nominal, current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=+0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3A-Grid - Both Pinned] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: 0.0 straight back)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd == 0.0, f"Expected 0.0 steer (straight back) when both sides are pinned, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Case 3B: Free Reverse Breadcrumb Tracking (> 3cm) with Inverted Steering Law
    tofs_open = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.20,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.20,
    }
    # Corridor target is to the Left (target_breadcrumb_yaw = +0.5 rad, current_yaw = 0.0 -> e_psi > 0)
    # When v < 0: steer_sign = -sign(e_psi) = -1.0 (steer RIGHT) -> nose swings LEFT
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016,
        current_v=-1.0,
        commanded_v=-1.0,
        cached_tofs=tofs_open,
        current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=+0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3B - Free Reverse Left Target] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: < 0 inverted sign)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd < 0.0, f"Expected inverted negative steer for positive heading error in reverse, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Corridor target is to the Right (target_breadcrumb_yaw = -0.5 rad, current_yaw = 0.0 -> e_psi < 0)
    # When v < 0: steer_sign = -sign(e_psi) = +1.0 (steer LEFT) -> nose swings RIGHT
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016,
        current_v=-1.0,
        commanded_v=-1.0,
        cached_tofs=tofs_open,
        current_yaw=0.0,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=-0.5,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3B - Free Reverse Right Target] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Target: > 0 inverted sign)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert steer_cmd > 0.0, f"Expected inverted positive steer for negative heading error in reverse, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Case 3C: Reverse vs U-Turn Arbitration (Front blocked -> PIVOT_REVERSE; Front open facing wrong way -> CORRECTIVE_180 U-Turn)
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE
    tofs_wall_blocked = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.10,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.10,
    }
    rec.trigger_emergency_recovery(
        heading_err=0.0,
        current_v=0.0,
        front_clearance_m=0.10,
        cached_tofs=tofs_wall_blocked,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=0.0,
        current_yaw=math.pi,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3C - Front Blocked Reverse] Trigger State: {rec.state} (Expected: PIVOT_REVERSE, v_cmd < 0)")
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE, f"Expected PIVOT_REVERSE, got {rec.state}"
    assert rec.current_cmd_v < 0.0, f"Expected negative reverse velocity, got {rec.current_cmd_v}"

    # Verify that while in PIVOT_REVERSE with front blocked, trigger_corrective_180 does not override reverse
    rec.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, cached_tofs=tofs_wall_blocked, front_clearance_m=0.10)
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE, f"Reverse overridden when blocked! State: {rec.state}"
    print("  [Case 3C - Blocked Reverse Immunity] trigger_corrective_180 blocked while front obstructed")

    # Verify that when front is open and facing wrong way, emergency recovery triggers forward U-turn (CORRECTIVE_180)
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE
    tofs_wide_open = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.60,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.60,
    }
    rec.trigger_emergency_recovery(
        heading_err=0.0,
        current_v=0.0,
        front_clearance_m=0.60,
        cached_tofs=tofs_wide_open,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=0.0,
        current_yaw=math.pi,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3C - Open Front U-Turn] Trigger State: {rec.state}, Substate: {rec.corrective_substate} (Expected: CORRECTIVE_180 FORWARD, v_cmd > 0)")
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.CORRECTIVE_180, f"Expected CORRECTIVE_180, got {rec.state}"
    assert rec.corrective_substate == "FORWARD", f"Expected substate FORWARD, got {rec.corrective_substate}"
    assert rec.current_cmd_v > 0.0, f"Expected positive forward velocity, got {rec.current_cmd_v}"

    # Verify that if in PIVOT_REVERSE and front opens up, trigger_corrective_180 transitions to U-turn
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, cached_tofs=tofs_wide_open, front_clearance_m=0.60)
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.CORRECTIVE_180, f"Expected CORRECTIVE_180, got {rec.state}"
    assert rec.corrective_substate == "FORWARD", f"Expected substate FORWARD, got {rec.corrective_substate}"
    print("  [Case 3C - Open Front Overrides Reverse] Successfully transitioned from PIVOT_REVERSE to U-turn")

    # Verify that while in CORRECTIVE_180, hitting a wall engages reverse support phase
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE
    rec.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, front_clearance_m=0.50)
    tofs_wall_blocked = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.10,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.10,
    }
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016,
        current_v=0.05,
        commanded_v=1.20,
        cached_tofs=tofs_wall_blocked,
        current_yaw=math.pi,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=0.0,
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3C - Wall Collision in 180] State: {rec.state}, Substate: {rec.corrective_substate}, target_v: {v_cmd:.2f}")
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.CORRECTIVE_180, f"Expected state CORRECTIVE_180, got {rec.state}"
    assert rec.corrective_substate == "REVERSE", f"Expected substate REVERSE, got {rec.corrective_substate}"
    assert v_cmd < 0.0, f"Expected negative reverse velocity, got {v_cmd}"

    # Case 3D: Open Side Resolution for CORRECTIVE_180
    # Test 1: Left arc more open
    dir_l = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.50, 0.15, 0.20))
    assert dir_l == 1.0, f"Expected +1.0 (Left), got {dir_l}"
    # Test 2: Right arc more open
    dir_r = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.15, 0.50, 0.20))
    assert dir_r == -1.0, f"Expected -1.0 (Right), got {dir_r}"
    # Test 3: Arcs symmetric, left side pinned by wall (<= 10cm) -> choose Right
    tofs_pinned_l = {"tof_side_left_90deg": 0.08, "tof_side_right_90deg": 0.40}
    dir_tofs_r = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.50, 0.50, 0.50), cached_tofs=tofs_pinned_l)
    assert dir_tofs_r == -1.0, f"Expected -1.0 (Right), got {dir_tofs_r}"
    # Test 4: Arcs symmetric, right side pinned by wall (<= 10cm) -> choose Left
    tofs_pinned_r = {"tof_side_left_90deg": 0.40, "tof_side_right_90deg": 0.08}
    dir_tofs_l = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.50, 0.50, 0.50), cached_tofs=tofs_pinned_r)
    assert dir_tofs_l == 1.0, f"Expected +1.0 (Left), got {dir_tofs_l}"
    # Test 5: 16-ch rig cross-pointing: Left open (1.2m), Right blocked by wall (0.12m via tof_front_left_82.9deg) -> choose Left (+1.0)
    tofs_16ch_right_wall = {
        "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 0.12,   # points Right into wall
        "tof_front_right_82.9deg": 1.20,  # points Left into open track
    }
    dir_16ch_l = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.50, 0.50, 0.50), cached_tofs=tofs_16ch_right_wall)
    assert dir_16ch_l == 1.0, f"Expected +1.0 (Left), got {dir_16ch_l}"
    # Test 6: 16-ch rig cross-pointing: Right open (1.2m), Left blocked by wall (0.12m via tof_front_right_82.9deg) -> choose Right (-1.0)
    tofs_16ch_left_wall = {
        "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 1.20,   # points Right into open track
        "tof_front_right_82.9deg": 0.12,  # points Left into wall
    }
    dir_16ch_r = StuckRecoveryManager.resolve_open_side(arc_clearance=(0.50, 0.50, 0.50), cached_tofs=tofs_16ch_left_wall)
    assert dir_16ch_r == -1.0, f"Expected -1.0 (Right), got {dir_16ch_r}"
    print("  [Case 3D - Open Side Selection] PASSED: Successfully resolves more open side via arcs and ToFs")

    # Case 3E: CORRECTIVE_180 Reverse-to-Forward Recovery Transition
    # Vehicle reverses for min reverse time, clearance opens up (>= 0.35m)
    tofs_open = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.45,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.45,
    }
    rec.corrective_reverse_timer = 0.30  # simulate elapsed reverse time >= RECOVERY_EXIT_MIN_TIME_S
    is_rec, v_cmd_fwd, steer_cmd_fwd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016,
        current_v=-0.80,
        commanded_v=-1.20,
        cached_tofs=tofs_open,
        current_yaw=math.pi,
        target_breadcrumb_yaw=0.0,
    )
    assert rec.corrective_substate == "FORWARD", f"Expected return to FORWARD substate, got {rec.corrective_substate}"
    assert v_cmd_fwd > 0.0, f"Expected positive forward velocity after clearance opened, got {v_cmd_fwd}"
    print("  [Case 3E - Reverse Support Resumes Forward 180] PASSED: Resumes forward 180 after clearance achieved")

    # Case 3F: Aligned reverse NEVER steers 0 (steers away from wall / towards open side)
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.stroke_timer = 0.05
    rec.preferred_yaw_sign = -1.0  # open side right
    tofs_mid = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.15,
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.15,
    }
    # Aligned: target_breadcrumb_yaw = 0.0, current_yaw = 0.0
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-0.5, commanded_v=-1.0, cached_tofs=tofs_mid, current_yaw=0.0, target_breadcrumb_yaw=0.0
    )
    print(f"  [Case 3F - Aligned Reverse Never Steers 0] Steer: {steer_cmd:+.3f} rad (Expected non-zero: abs > 0)")
    assert is_rec is True
    assert abs(steer_cmd) > 0.1, f"Expected non-zero steering during aligned reverse, got {steer_cmd}"

    # Case 3G: Clearance Exit (Front >= 0.25m AND Any ToF >= 0.60m) & Zero-Steer Handover Handshake
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.stroke_timer = 0.10
    tofs_cleared = {
        "tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.30,   # >= 0.25m front
        "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.30,  # >= 0.25m front
        "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 0.65,  # >= 0.60m any-ToF
    }
    # Step 1: Clearance triggers DECEL_AND_CENTER; physical active_steer is still turned (0.40 rad)
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-0.5, commanded_v=-1.0, cached_tofs=tofs_cleared, active_steer=0.40, current_yaw=0.0
    )
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.DECEL_AND_CENTER, f"Expected DECEL_AND_CENTER, got {rec.state}"
    assert is_rec is True, "Recovery manager must maintain control while wheels are centering"
    assert v_cmd == 0.0, f"Expected 0.0 speed commanded during centering, got {v_cmd}"
    print(f"  [Case 3G - Handshake Holding] State: {rec.state}, v_cmd: {v_cmd}, is_recovering: {is_rec}")

    # Step 2: Wheels finish slewing back to center (active_steer = 0.01 rad)
    rec.current_cmd_steer = 0.0
    is_rec, v_cmd, steer_cmd = rec.update(
        dt=0.016, current_v=-0.05, commanded_v=0.0, cached_tofs=tofs_cleared, active_steer=0.01, current_yaw=0.0
    )
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE, f"Expected NORMAL_DRIVE after wheels centered, got {rec.state}"
    assert is_rec is False, "Expected is_recovering = False cleanly handing off to planner"
    print(f"  [Case 3G - Handshake Release] State: {rec.state}, is_recovering: {is_rec} (Clean handoff to planner)")

    print("  -> Smart Reverse Recovery: PASSED (Kinematics, Open Side, Non-Zero Aligned Steer & Handshake 100% verified)")


def test_unified_coordinator_and_benchmark():
    print("\n--- Testing Unified Coordinator & Latency Benchmark ---")
    watchdog = AntiWrongWayWatchdog(mode=WrongWayOptionMode.OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB)

    # 1. Simulate 500 steps of normal straight driving
    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    for i in range(500):
        t = i * 0.0083  # 120 Hz
        x = 0.0
        y = i * 0.02
        yaw = 0.0
        status = watchdog.update(x, y, yaw, v_est=2.4, yaw_rate=0.01, now_s=t)
        assert status.is_wrong_way is False, "Breadcrumb watchdog false positive during straight driving!"

    elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000.0
    avg_latency_us = (elapsed_ms / 500.0) * 1000.0
    print(f"  500 Cycles Execution Time: {elapsed_ms:.2f} ms ({avg_latency_us:.1f} microseconds per 120 Hz cycle)")
    assert avg_latency_us < 250.0, f"Latency {avg_latency_us:.1f} us exceeds 250 us budget!"

    # 2. Test in-lane reversal event (must satisfy confirmation threshold >= 5 consecutive frames)
    t_now = 500 * 0.0083
    # Vehicle spins to 180 degrees at current x=0.0, y=10.0
    for step in range(6):
        t_now += 0.0083
        rev_status = watchdog.update(0.0, 10.0, math.pi, v_est=0.1, yaw_rate=4.0, now_s=t_now)
    print(f"  Watchdog Response on Reversal: Detected = {rev_status.is_wrong_way}, By = {rev_status.detected_by_option}, Action = {rev_status.corrective_action}")
    assert rev_status.is_wrong_way is True, "Watchdog failed to trigger on wrong-way reversal!"
    assert rev_status.detected_by_option == "OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB", "Expected detection by OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB"
    assert rev_status.corrective_action in ("CORRECTIVE_180", "RESTRICT_STEERING"), "Expected corrective action!"

    print("  -> Unified Coordinator & Benchmark: PASSED (< 0.25 ms compute budget confirmed)")


def test_early_gap_exit_sentry():
    print("\n--- Testing Narrow-Cone Early Gap Exit Sentry ---")
    watchdog = AntiWrongWayWatchdog(mode=WrongWayOptionMode.OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB)

    # 1. Simulate forward driving along +Y from Y=0 to Y=6.0 (heading = 0.0)
    for i in range(120):
        t = i * 0.05
        watchdog.update(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, v_est=2.0, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=t)

    # Vehicle is at (0.0, 6.0) facing wrong way (yaw = pi)
    curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw = 0.0, 6.0, math.pi

    # Test 1A: Gap candidate directly into historical trail (in robot frame: xg=0.0, yg=1.5m -> points south back to Y=4.5)
    # Old breadcrumbs at Y=4.5 had yaw=0.0 (opposing 180° to current yaw=pi)
    has_opp, count, bcs = watchdog.check_gap_channel(curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.5)
    is_exit, exit_info = watchdog.is_gap_early_exit(curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.5)
    print(f"  [Test 1A - Gap Into Old Trail] Opposing: {has_opp} (count={count}), Early Exit: {is_exit}")
    assert has_opp is True, "Expected opposing breadcrumbs in channel pointing along old trail"
    assert is_exit is False, "Candidate gap pointing along old trail must NOT qualify as early exit"
    assert exit_info["reason"] == "OPPOSING_BREADCRUMBS_IN_CHANNEL"

    # Test 1B: Purely geometric obstacle immunity (no raycasting failure)
    # Even if an obstacle/pillar was placed between robot and trail, check_gap_channel is immune to LOS occlusion
    assert has_opp is True, "Narrow cone check must be purely spatial/geometric and immune to LOS raycast failure"

    # Test 1C: Gap pointing into fresh open track (xg=1.2, yg=1.2, d=1.70m, angle=+45° where no breadcrumbs exist)
    has_opp_clear, count_clear, _ = watchdog.check_gap_channel(curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw, gap_xg=1.2, gap_yg=1.2)
    is_exit_clear, exit_info_clear = watchdog.is_gap_early_exit(curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw, gap_xg=1.2, gap_yg=1.2)
    print(f"  [Test 1C - Gap Into Clear Track] Opposing: {has_opp_clear}, Early Exit: {is_exit_clear}")
    assert has_opp_clear is False, "Expected no opposing breadcrumbs in clear channel"
    assert is_exit_clear is True, "Candidate gap pointing into fresh track must qualify as early exit"
    assert exit_info_clear["reason"] == "SAFE_EARLY_EXIT"

    # Test 1D: Gap pointing into fresh track but too short (< 1.0m, e.g. d=0.7m)
    is_exit_short, exit_info_short = watchdog.is_gap_early_exit(curr_x, curr_y, curr_yaw, gap_xg=0.5, gap_yg=0.5)
    print(f"  [Test 1D - Gap < 1.0m] Early Exit: {is_exit_short} (reason={exit_info_short['reason']})")
    assert is_exit_short is False, "Gap < 1.0m must not qualify as long-path early exit"
    assert exit_info_short["reason"] == "GAP_TOO_SHORT"

    # Test 1E: Narrow cone points along candidate GAP VECTOR, NOT robot heading
    # Create breadcrumb at +15 deg from robot heading (+X=0.26m, +Y=1.0m in vehicle frame)
    # 1. Candidate gap at 0 deg (robot heading): ±10 deg cone does NOT include +15 deg breadcrumb!
    # 2. Candidate gap at +15 deg: ±10 deg cone DOES include +15 deg breadcrumb!
    bc_watchdog = BreadcrumbWatchdog()
    # Add dummy history point for odometry gate
    bc_watchdog.cumulative_dist_m = 5.0
    # Add breadcrumb at vehicle frame xv=0.26, yv=1.0 (angle = atan2(0.26, 1.0) = ~14.6 deg)
    # If robot is at (0,0) yaw=0: xv=dx, yv=dy -> breadcrumb at (0.26, 1.0), yaw=pi (opposing)
    bc_watchdog.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.26, y=1.0, yaw=math.pi, v=2.0, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=1.0))
    # Must have >= 5 points for watchdog evaluation
    for k in range(5):
        bc_watchdog.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=10.0 + k, y=10.0, yaw=0.0, v=2.0, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=0.1))

    # Gap at heading (0 deg): (0.0, 1.2) -> breadcrumb at 14.6 deg is OUTSIDE 20 deg cone (half-angle 10 deg)
    has_opp_fwd, _, _ = bc_watchdog.check_gap_channel(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.2, cone_deg=20.0)
    # Gap at +15 deg: (0.31, 1.16) -> breadcrumb at 14.6 deg is INSIDE cone centered on gap!
    has_opp_ang, _, _ = bc_watchdog.check_gap_channel(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, gap_xg=0.31, gap_yg=1.16, cone_deg=20.0)
    print(f"  [Test 1E - Cone Oriented Along Gap Vector] Forward Gap Opposing: {has_opp_fwd}, Angled Gap Opposing: {has_opp_ang}")
    assert has_opp_fwd is False, "Breadcrumb at 14.6 deg should be outside the forward gap cone"
    assert has_opp_ang is True, "Breadcrumb at 14.6 deg must be inside the cone centered on the angled gap vector"

    # Test 1F: Recovery Early Exit Termination for CORRECTIVE_180 (once chassis has turned >= 95 deg or is within 55 deg of target)
    rec = StuckRecoveryManager()
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.CORRECTIVE_180
    rec.corrective_substate = "FORWARD"
    rec.target_corrective_yaw = math.pi
    rec.last_corrective_yaw = math.radians(150.0)
    # Provide open front ToF (d_straight = 1.0m)
    tofs_open = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 1.0, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 1.0}
    is_rec, _, _ = rec.update(dt=0.016, current_v=1.0, cached_tofs=tofs_open, current_yaw=math.radians(150.0), early_exit_available=True)
    print(f"  [Test 1F - 180 Early Abort] State: {rec.state}, is_recovering: {is_rec}")
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE, "CORRECTIVE_180 must return to NORMAL_DRIVE on early exit with open front"
    assert is_rec is False, "is_recovering must be False after early exit finish"

    # Test 1G: PIVOT_REVERSE is IMMUNE to early exit abort (must keep reversing away from wall!)
    rec.state = StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    rec.stroke_timer = 0.05
    tofs_wall = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.01, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.01}
    is_rec_rev, v_cmd_rev, _ = rec.update(dt=0.016, current_v=-0.5, cached_tofs=tofs_wall, early_exit_available=True)
    print(f"  [Test 1G - PIVOT_REVERSE Immunity] State: {rec.state}, v_cmd: {v_cmd_rev:+.2f} m/s, is_recovering: {is_rec_rev}")
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE, "PIVOT_REVERSE must NOT abort when early_exit_available is passed!"
    assert is_rec_rev is True, "Robot must continue reversing when in PIVOT_REVERSE"
    assert v_cmd_rev < 0.0, "Expected negative velocity command during reverse"

    # Test 1H: Close-range breadcrumbs starting from 15cm out (e.g. at 20cm) block early exit
    bc_dog_close = BreadcrumbWatchdog()
    bc_dog_close.cumulative_dist_m = 2.0
    # Breadcrumb at y = +0.20m in front of vehicle (xv=0.0, yv=0.20m), opposing heading
    bc_dog_close.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=0.20, yaw=math.pi, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=1.5))
