    for k in range(5):
        bc_dog_close.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=20.0 + k, y=20.0, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=0.1))

    # Candidate gap at (0.0, 1.5m). With min_bc_dist=0.15m:
    # 20cm breadcrumb (0.15m <= d=0.20m < 0.30m) MUST be detected and block early exit!
    has_opp_close, cnt_close, _ = bc_dog_close.check_gap_channel(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.5)
    is_exit_close, exit_info_close = bc_dog_close.is_gap_early_exit(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.5)
    print(f"  [Test 1H - Close Breadcrumb @ 20cm] Opposing: {has_opp_close} (count={cnt_close}), Early Exit: {is_exit_close}")
    assert has_opp_close is True, "Breadcrumb at 20cm must be detected by early exit sentry starting from 15cm out"
    assert is_exit_close is False, "Early exit must be BLOCKED when opposing breadcrumb exists 20cm out"
    assert exit_info_close["reason"] == "OPPOSING_BREADCRUMBS_IN_CHANNEL"

    # Breadcrumb inside < 15cm (e.g. 10cm under chassis center) is excluded
    bc_dog_too_close = BreadcrumbWatchdog()
    bc_dog_too_close.cumulative_dist_m = 2.0
    bc_dog_too_close.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=0.10, yaw=math.pi, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=1.5))
    for k in range(5):
        bc_dog_too_close.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=20.0 + k, y=20.0, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=0.1))
    has_opp_sub15, _, _ = bc_dog_too_close.check_gap_channel(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, gap_xg=0.0, gap_yg=1.5)
    assert has_opp_sub15 is False, "Breadcrumb at 10cm (< 15cm) must be excluded"

    # Test 1I: Early exit must NOT suppress wrong-way when forward Line-of-Sight sees opposing breadcrumbs
    watchdog_ww = AntiWrongWayWatchdog(mode=WrongWayOptionMode.OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB)
    for i in range(120):
        watchdog_ww.update(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, v_est=2.0, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=i * 0.05)
    # Vehicle is at (0.0, 6.0) facing wrong way (yaw = pi), seeing opposing trail along +Y
    # Provide candidate gap at an angle (e.g. +30 deg: gx=0.6, gy=1.2)
    st = None
    for k in range(watchdog_ww.CONFIRM_STEPS_THRESHOLD + 2):
        st = watchdog_ww.update(
            0.0, 6.0, math.pi, v_est=1.5, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=10.0 + k * 0.01,
            candidate_gaps=[(0.6, 1.2)], state="NORMAL_DRIVE"
        )
    print(f"  [Test 1I - No Early Exit Suppression While Facing Breadcrumbs] Wrong Way: {st.is_wrong_way}, Early Exit: {st.early_exit_found}")
    assert st.is_wrong_way is True, "Confirmed opposing LOS breadcrumbs must trigger wrong way and NOT be suppressed by angled candidate gap"
    assert st.early_exit_found is False, "Early exit must NOT trigger when forward LOS sees opposing breadcrumbs"

    print("  -> Narrow-Cone Early Gap Exit Sentry: PASSED (100% verified)")


def test_breadcrumb_duration_scale():
    print("\n--- Testing Breadcrumb Duration Scale & Max Timeout ---")
    assert AWW_BREADCRUMB_DURATION_SCALE == 0.35, f"Expected default scale 0.35x, got {AWW_BREADCRUMB_DURATION_SCALE}"
    assert AWW_BREADCRUMB_MAX_TIMEOUT_S == 6.0, f"Expected default max timeout 6.0s, got {AWW_BREADCRUMB_MAX_TIMEOUT_S}"

    # 1. Compare 1.0x baseline vs 0.6x faster disappearance
    dog_10 = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, duration_scale=1.0)
    dog_06 = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, duration_scale=0.6)

    assert dog_10.effective_lookback_m == 8.0
    assert abs(dog_06.effective_lookback_m - 4.8) < 1e-4, f"Expected 4.8m effective lookback, got {dog_06.effective_lookback_m}"

    # Simulate racing speed drive: robot drives 7.0 meters (from Y=0.0 to Y=7.0 at 2.0 m/s, taking 3.5s <= 6.0s timeout)
    # Track perimeter is ~6.0m, so at 7.0m it has completed a full lap
    t = 0.0
    for i in range(141):
        y = i * 0.05  # up to 7.0m
        dog_10.add_point(0.0, y, yaw=0.0, v=2.0, now_s=t, state="DRIVE")
        dog_06.add_point(0.0, y, yaw=0.0, v=2.0, now_s=t, state="DRIVE")
        t += 0.025  # 140 * 0.025 = 3.5s total (within 6.0s timeout)

    # In dog_10 (8.0m lookback):
    # Total distance is 7.0m <= 8.0m, so breadcrumb from start of lap (Y=0.0) is STILL RETAINED!
    oldest_10 = dog_10.breadcrumbs[0].y
    print(f"  [1.0x Scale] Oldest Breadcrumb at Y = {oldest_10:.2f} m (Lap Start at Y=0.0m is STILL RETAINED)")
    assert oldest_10 == 0.0, "Baseline 1.0x should retain start of lap across 7m track"

    # In dog_06 (4.8m lookback, 0.6x):
    # Distance window is 4.8m, so points older than 7.0 - 4.8 = 2.2m have disappeared!
    oldest_06 = dog_06.breadcrumbs[0].y
    print(f"  [0.6x Scale] Oldest Breadcrumb at Y = {oldest_06:.2f} m (Lap Start at Y=0.0m has DISAPPEARED)")
    assert oldest_06 >= 2.15, f"Expected oldest point >= 2.15m (start of lap pruned), got {oldest_06}"

    # Test 6.0s timeout expires stale breadcrumbs during prolonged standstill (> 6.0s)
    dog_timeout = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, duration_scale=1.0, max_timeout_s=6.0)
    dog_timeout.add_point(0.0, 0.0, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, now_s=0.0, state="DRIVE")
    dog_timeout.add_point(0.0, 0.1, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, now_s=0.1, state="DRIVE")
    # Prolonged standstill for 6.5s
    dog_timeout.add_point(0.0, 0.2, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, now_s=6.6, state="DRIVE")
    # First point at t=0.0 must be expired (6.6s > 6.0s timeout)
    assert dog_timeout.breadcrumbs[0].timestamp >= 0.1, "Point at t=0.0 should expire after >6.0s timeout"
    print("  [6.0s Timeout] Verified: Points older than 6.0s timeout safely expire during prolonged standstill")

    # 2. Dynamic runtime update via set_duration_scale
    dog_06.set_duration_scale(0.4)
    assert abs(dog_06.effective_lookback_m - 3.2) < 1e-4

    # 3. Watchdog coordinator interface & detailed telemetry check
    watchdog = AntiWrongWayWatchdog(duration_scale=0.6)
    assert watchdog.breadcrumb_watchdog.duration_scale == 0.6
    watchdog.set_duration_scale(0.5)
    assert watchdog.breadcrumb_watchdog.duration_scale == 0.5

    telem = watchdog.get_detailed_telemetry(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, v_est=1.0, yaw_rate=0.0)
    assert "duration_scale" in telem["opt1"]
    assert telem["opt1"]["duration_scale"] == 0.5
    assert "0.50x" in telem["opt1"]["thought"]

    print("  -> Breadcrumb Duration Scale: PASSED (0.6x parameter verified, prevents full-lap overlap on small tracks)")


def test_regression_fixes():
    print("\n--- Testing Wrong-Way & Recovery Regression Fixes ---")
    grid_h = int(round((2.60 - GRID_Y_MIN) / GRID_RES)) + 1
    grid_w = int(round((1.25 - GRID_X_MIN) / GRID_RES)) + 1

    # R1: unknown_mask blocks sightline across unmapped region
    dog = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, spacing_m=0.05)
    for i in range(60):
        dog.add_point(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, 1.5, i * 0.03, state="DRIVE")
    haz_clear = np.zeros((grid_h, grid_w), dtype=np.uint8)
    unk_wall = np.zeros((grid_h, grid_w), dtype=np.uint8)
    unk_wall[30:40, :] = 1
    is_ww_unk, _, _, _ = dog.check_wrong_way(0.0, 3.0, math.pi, hazard_mask=haz_clear, unknown_mask=unk_wall)
    assert is_ww_unk is False, "unknown_mask band must block LOS sightline!"

    # R2: Single leaked breadcrumb (< 3) does NOT trip; cluster >= 3 DOES trip with circular mean
    dog_cluster = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, spacing_m=0.05, min_confirmed=3)
    for i in range(5):
        dog_cluster.add_point(10.0, 10.0 + i * 0.1, 0.0, 1.5, i * 0.05, state="DRIVE")
    dog_cluster.cumulative_dist_m = 5.0
    dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.clear()
    # 5 points, only 1 in-cone (rest out of bounds), -> must NOT trip with min_confirmed=3
    for k in range(4):
        dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=5.0, y=5.0, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=1.0))
    dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=1.0, yaw=0.0, v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=3.0))
    is_ww_single, _, _, _ = dog_cluster.check_wrong_way(0.0, 2.0, math.pi, hazard_mask=haz_clear)
    assert is_ww_single is False, "Single leaked breadcrumb must not trip min_confirmed=3!"

    # Add 2 more opposing points with yaws around +/- pi (circular mean test near pi)
    dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=0.9, yaw=math.radians(175.0), v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=2.9))
    dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=0.8, yaw=math.radians(-175.0), v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=2.8))
    dog_cluster.breadcrumbs.append(Breadcrumb(x=0.0, y=0.7, yaw=math.radians(180.0), v=1.5, timestamp=1.0, s_dist=2.7))
    # Robot at (0.0, 0.0) facing 0.0 (+Y), looking at opposing cluster at y=0.7..1.0 (opposing ~pi)
    is_ww_multi, _, tgt_mean, _ = dog_cluster.check_wrong_way(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, hazard_mask=haz_clear)
    assert is_ww_multi is True, "Cluster >= 3 must trip!"
    assert abs(abs(tgt_mean) - math.pi) < 0.15, f"Expected circular mean near +-pi, got {tgt_mean}"

    # R3: Debounce counter capped & reset across CORRECTIVE_180 + post-recovery cooldown
    aww = AntiWrongWayWatchdog(mode=WrongWayOptionMode.OPTION_1_BREADCRUMB)
    for i in range(60):
        aww.update(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, v_est=2.0, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=i * 0.02, dt=0.02)
    for k in range(30):
        aww.update(0.0, 3.0, math.pi, v_est=0.2, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=2.0 + k * 0.016, hazard_mask=haz_clear, state="DRIVE", dt=0.016)
    assert aww.consecutive_ww_steps == aww.CONFIRM_STEPS_THRESHOLD, f"Debounce counter leaked above cap: {aww.consecutive_ww_steps}"
    # Enter and exit CORRECTIVE_180 -> must reset counter & start cooldown
    aww.update(0.0, 3.0, math.pi, v_est=0.8, yaw_rate=2.0, now_s=2.5, hazard_mask=haz_clear, state="CORRECTIVE_180", dt=0.016)
    assert aww.consecutive_ww_steps == 0, "Entering CORRECTIVE_180 must reset consecutive_ww_steps"
    st_cool = aww.update(0.0, 3.0, math.pi, v_est=0.8, yaw_rate=0.0, now_s=2.52, hazard_mask=haz_clear, state="DRIVE", dt=0.016)
    assert st_cool.is_wrong_way is False and aww.post_recovery_cooldown_s > 0.0, "Post-recovery cooldown must suppress immediate re-trigger"

    # R4: Unsigned wiggle (+-70 deg) does NOT falsely exit; signed alignment during REVERSE DOES exit
    rec = StuckRecoveryManager()
    rec.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, front_clearance_m=0.80)
    tofs_open = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.80, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.80}
    # Wiggle +70 deg then -70 deg (net 0 deg)
    rec.update(dt=0.016, current_v=0.8, cached_tofs=tofs_open, current_yaw=wrap_to_pi(math.pi + math.radians(70.0)))
    rec.update(dt=0.016, current_v=0.8, cached_tofs=tofs_open, current_yaw=math.pi)
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.CORRECTIVE_180, "Unsigned wiggle must NOT exit CORRECTIVE_180!"

    # Signed alignment during REVERSE -> exits cleanly to DECEL_AND_CENTER
    rec.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, front_clearance_m=0.10)
    assert rec.corrective_substate == "REVERSE"
    tofs_rev_done = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.50, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.50, "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 0.80}
    rec.update(dt=0.016, current_v=-0.5, cached_tofs=tofs_rev_done, current_yaw=math.radians(15.0))
    assert rec.state == StuckRecoveryState.DECEL_AND_CENTER, f"Aligned REVERSE should enter DECEL_AND_CENTER, got {rec.state}"

    # R5: check_gap_channel checks gap's world heading (rejects wrong-way angled gap mid-turn)
    bc_gap = BreadcrumbWatchdog()
    for i in range(60):
        bc_gap.add_point(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, 2.0, i * 0.05, state="DRIVE")
    # Robot at (0.0, 3.0) mid-turn facing yaw = +pi/2 (Left, -X), gap angled -90 deg left (xg=-1.5, yg=0.01 -> points south along old +Y trail!)
    # Chassis yaw (+pi/2) vs trail yaw (0.0) is only 90 deg (< 110 deg), BUT gap_world_yaw (~pi) vs trail yaw (0.0) is 180 deg!
    has_opp_gap, _, _ = bc_gap.check_gap_channel(0.0, 3.0, math.pi / 2.0, gap_xg=-1.5, gap_yg=0.01)
    assert has_opp_gap is True, "check_gap_channel must check gap_world_yaw and catch wrong-way gap mid-turn!"

    # R6: Corner-catch stall (v = 0, d_straight = 0.35m) triggers reverse support
    rec_stall = StuckRecoveryManager()
    rec_stall.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, front_clearance_m=0.50)
    rec_stall.forward_grace_timer = 0.0
    rec_stall.stroke_timer = 0.30
    tofs_corner = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.35, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.35}
    rec_stall.update(dt=0.016, current_v=0.02, cached_tofs=tofs_corner, current_yaw=math.pi)
    assert rec_stall.corrective_substate == "REVERSE", "Corner-catch stall with open straight ToF must trigger REVERSE!"

    # R7: Offline 0.0 ToF sentinel does NOT trigger false pinned state
    tofs_offline = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.0, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.50}
    summary = StuckRecoveryManager.parse_cached_tofs(tofs_offline)
    assert summary.d_straight == 0.50, f"Offline 0.0 sentinel leaked into d_straight: {summary.d_straight}"
    is_l_p, is_r_p = rec_stall.evaluate_pinned_state(None, None, tofs_offline)
    assert is_l_p is False and is_r_p is False, "Offline 0.0 sentinel must not trigger pinned state!"

    # R8: Standstill > 6s expires stale breadcrumbs
    dog_stale = BreadcrumbWatchdog(max_timeout_s=6.0)
    for i in range(10):
        dog_stale.add_point(0.0, i * 0.1, 0.0, 1.5, i * 0.05, state="DRIVE")
    dog_stale.add_point(0.0, 0.9, 0.0, 0.0, 10.0, state="DRIVE")
    assert len(dog_stale.breadcrumbs) == 0, "Standstill > 6s in DRIVE must expire stale breadcrumbs!"

    # R9: Diagonal 135-deg spin in same corridor triggers, while parallel hairpin lane (1.0m offset) is rejected
    dog_diag = BreadcrumbWatchdog(lookback_m=8.0, spacing_m=0.05)
    for i in range(41):
        dog_diag.add_point(0.0, i * 0.05, 0.0, 1.5, i * 0.03, state="DRIVE")
    is_ww_diag, _, _, _ = dog_diag.check_wrong_way(0.0, 2.0, math.radians(135.0), hazard_mask=haz_clear)
    assert is_ww_diag is True, "Diagonal 135-deg spin in same corridor must detect wrong way!"
    is_ww_adj_diag, _, _, _ = dog_diag.check_wrong_way(1.0, 2.0, math.radians(135.0), hazard_mask=haz_clear)
    assert is_ww_adj_diag is False, "Adjacent hairpin lane at 1.0m lateral offset must still be rejected!"

    # R10: Reverse stall detection in PIVOT_REVERSE triggers WIGGLE_ESCAPE with alternating fwd/rev + steer
    rec_wig = StuckRecoveryManager()
    tofs_wedged = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.08, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.08, "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 0.15}
    rec_wig.trigger_emergency_recovery(heading_err=0.0, current_v=0.0, front_clearance_m=0.08, cached_tofs=tofs_wedged)
    assert rec_wig.state == StuckRecoveryState.PIVOT_REVERSE
    dt = 0.02
    entered_wiggle = False
    seen_cmds = []
    for _ in range(80):  # up to 1.6s
        is_rec, v_c, s_c = rec_wig.update(dt=dt, current_v=0.0, cached_tofs=tofs_wedged)
        if rec_wig.state == StuckRecoveryState.WIGGLE_ESCAPE:
            entered_wiggle = True
            seen_cmds.append((v_c > 0.0, s_c > 0.0))
    assert entered_wiggle, f"Reverse stall must enter WIGGLE_ESCAPE, got {rec_wig.state}"
    assert any(v_pos for v_pos, _ in seen_cmds) and any(not v_pos for v_pos, _ in seen_cmds), (
        f"WIGGLE_ESCAPE must alternate forward and reverse pulses, saw {set(seen_cmds)}"
    )
    assert any(s_pos for _, s_pos in seen_cmds) and any(not s_pos for _, s_pos in seen_cmds), (
        f"WIGGLE_ESCAPE must alternate left and right steering, saw {set(seen_cmds)}"
    )

    # R11: WIGGLE_ESCAPE exits cleanly when clearance opens, and CORRECTIVE_180[REVERSE] stall with blocked front enters WIGGLE_ESCAPE
    tofs_freed = {"tof_front_left_4.1deg": 0.50, "tof_front_right_4.1deg": 0.50, "tof_front_left_82.9deg": 0.80}
    rec_wig._enter_wiggle_escape()
    rec_wig._wiggle_phase_timer = 0.12
    is_rec_done, _, _ = rec_wig.update(dt=0.02, current_v=0.35, cached_tofs=tofs_freed)
    assert rec_wig.state == StuckRecoveryState.NORMAL_DRIVE and not is_rec_done, (
        f"WIGGLE_ESCAPE should finish cleanly when front opens, got {rec_wig.state}"
    )

    rec_180_wig = StuckRecoveryManager()
    rec_180_wig.trigger_corrective_180(target_yaw=0.0, steer_sign=1.0, current_yaw=math.pi, cached_tofs=tofs_wedged, front_clearance_m=0.08)
    assert rec_180_wig.corrective_substate == "REVERSE"
    for _ in range(30):  # 0.60s
        rec_180_wig.update(dt=0.02, current_v=0.0, cached_tofs=tofs_wedged, current_yaw=math.pi)
        if rec_180_wig.state == StuckRecoveryState.WIGGLE_ESCAPE:
            break
    assert rec_180_wig.state == StuckRecoveryState.WIGGLE_ESCAPE, (
        f"CORRECTIVE_180[REVERSE] stall with blocked front must enter WIGGLE_ESCAPE, got {rec_180_wig.state}"
    )

    print("  -> Regression Fixes (R1-R11): PASSED")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("================================================================================")
    print("  RUNNING ANTI-WRONG-WAY & U-TURN DISCRIMINATION TEST SUITE")
    print("================================================================================")
    test_option1_breadcrumb()
    test_breadcrumb_duration_scale()
    test_early_gap_exit_sentry()
    test_smart_reverse_recovery()
    test_unified_coordinator_and_benchmark()
    test_regression_fixes()
    print("\n================================================================================")
    print("  ALL TESTS PASSED SUCCESSFULLY! 100% PASS RATE ACROSS ALL MODES.")
    print("================================================================================")


=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/test_planners_offline.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  OFFLINE VALIDATION & BENCHMARK: C++ CURVILINEAR S-L MPC (OSQP)
================================================================================
  Self-contained test suite verifying:
    1. C++ Curvilinear S-L MPC DLL loading and QP initialization
    2. Left-hand target tracking and steering calculation
    3. Right-hand target tracking and steering calculation
    4. Slew-rate counter-steering recovery response
    5. Latency profiling over 100 runs (<2 ms target)
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import numpy as np

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CONTROLLERS_DIR = os.path.dirname(CURR_DIR)
PROJECT_DIR = os.path.dirname(CONTROLLERS_DIR)
WORKSPACE_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(PROJECT_DIR)))

for p in [CURR_DIR, CONTROLLERS_DIR, PROJECT_DIR, WORKSPACE_ROOT]:
    if p not in sys.path and os.path.exists(p):
        sys.path.insert(0, p)

from cpp_smart_hybrid_costmap import CppSmartHybridCostmap, SENSOR_NAMES
from planner_sl_mpc import CurvilinearSLMPC

cm = CppSmartHybridCostmap()
mpc = CurvilinearSLMPC(cm)

print("=" * 80)
print("TEST 1: TARGET ON LEFT (xg = -0.60m, yg = 1.20m), steer_current = 0.0")
print("=" * 80)
r1 = mpc.plan(xg=-0.6, yg=1.2, theta_g=-0.46, aperture_width=0.6, v_current=1.2, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
s1 = np.degrees(r1['cmd_steer_rad'])
p1 = r1['best_line'][1]
print(f"C++ SL-MPC: steer = {s1:+6.1f} deg | pt[1] = ({p1[0]:+.3f}, {p1[1]:.3f})")
assert r1['cmd_steer_rad'] > 0, "C++ SL-MPC should turn LEFT (positive steer)"

print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("TEST 2: TARGET ON RIGHT (xg = +0.60m, yg = 1.20m), steer_current = 0.0")
print("=" * 80)
mpc.reset_active_target()
r2 = mpc.plan(xg=0.6, yg=1.2, theta_g=0.46, aperture_width=0.6, v_current=1.2, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
s2 = np.degrees(r2['cmd_steer_rad'])
p2 = r2['best_line'][1]
print(f"C++ SL-MPC: steer = {s2:+6.1f} deg | pt[1] = ({p2[0]:+.3f}, {p2[1]:.3f})")
assert r2['cmd_steer_rad'] < 0, "C++ SL-MPC should turn RIGHT (negative steer)"

print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("TEST 3: COUNTER-STEER RECOVERY (Target Left, steer_current = -0.25 rad / -14.3 deg right)")
print("=" * 80)
mpc.reset_active_target()
r3 = mpc.plan(xg=-0.6, yg=1.2, theta_g=-0.46, aperture_width=0.6, v_current=1.2, steer_current=-0.25, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
s3 = np.degrees(r3['cmd_steer_rad'])
print(f"C++ SL-MPC: steer = {s3:+6.1f} deg (slew from -14.3 deg toward positive/left)")
assert r3['cmd_steer_rad'] > -0.25, "C++ SL-MPC should slew steer towards left (+)"

print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("TEST 4: CORRIDOR CENTERING BIAS (ASYMMETRIC WALLS)")
print("=" * 80)

# Matches SENSOR_RIG geometry in smart_hybrid_costmap_front.cpp (+Y forward, +X right)
_TOF_SENSOR_RIG = [
    ("tof_front_left_4.1deg",   -0.058, 0.092, 1.5512),
    ("tof_front_left_15.4deg",  -0.058, 0.092, 1.3548),
    ("tof_front_left_26.6deg",  -0.058, 0.092, 1.1585),
    ("tof_front_left_37.9deg",  -0.058, 0.092, 0.9621),
    ("tof_front_left_49.1deg",  -0.057, 0.090, 0.7658),
    ("tof_front_left_60.4deg",  -0.057, 0.090, 0.5694),
    ("tof_front_left_71.6deg",  -0.057, 0.090, 0.3731),
    ("tof_front_left_82.9deg",  -0.057, 0.090, 0.1767),
    ("tof_front_right_4.1deg",   0.058, 0.092, 1.5904),
    ("tof_front_right_15.4deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.7868),
    ("tof_front_right_26.6deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.9831),
    ("tof_front_right_37.9deg",  0.058, 0.092, 2.1795),
    ("tof_front_right_49.1deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.3758),
    ("tof_front_right_60.4deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.5722),
    ("tof_front_right_71.6deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.7685),
    ("tof_front_right_82.9deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.9649),
]


def _raycast_parallel_walls(x_left: float, x_right: float, max_range: float = 2.40) -> dict:
    readings = {}
    for name, x0, _y0, heading in _TOF_SENSOR_RIG:
        dx = float(np.cos(heading))
        t_hit = max_range
        if dx > 1e-6:
            t_hit = min(t_hit, (x_right - x0) / dx)
        elif dx < -1e-6:
            t_hit = min(t_hit, (x_left - x0) / dx)
        readings[name] = float(np.clip(t_hit, 0.001, max_range))
    return readings


def _populate_walls(costmap: CppSmartHybridCostmap, x_left: float, x_right: float, frames: int = 5) -> None:
    costmap.clear()
    scan = _raycast_parallel_walls(x_left, x_right)
    ordered = [scan[name] for name in SENSOR_NAMES]
    for _ in range(frames):
        costmap.update(ordered)


# Case A: Right wall close (+0.22m), left wall far (-0.78m) -> center_bias should steer LEFT (+steer, -X)
_populate_walls(cm, x_left=-0.78, x_right=0.22)
mpc.set_center_bias(0.0)
mpc.reset_active_target()
for _ in range(3):
    r_base_rclose = mpc.plan(xg=0.0, yg=1.2, theta_g=0.0, aperture_width=0.8, v_current=1.5, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
mpc.set_center_bias(1.0)
mpc.reset_active_target()
for _ in range(3):
    r_cent_rclose = mpc.plan(xg=0.0, yg=1.2, theta_g=0.0, aperture_width=0.8, v_current=1.5, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
print(
    f"Case A (right close): bias=0 steer={np.degrees(r_base_rclose['cmd_steer_rad']):+5.2f} deg end_x={r_base_rclose['best_line'][-1][0]:+.3f}m | "
    f"bias=1 steer={np.degrees(r_cent_rclose['cmd_steer_rad']):+5.2f} deg end_x={r_cent_rclose['best_line'][-1][0]:+.3f}m"
)
assert r_cent_rclose['cmd_steer_rad'] > r_base_rclose['cmd_steer_rad'] + 0.004, "Center bias should steer further LEFT away from close right wall"
assert r_cent_rclose['cmd_steer_rad'] > 0.0, "Center bias should produce positive (LEFT) steer when right wall is close"
assert r_cent_rclose['best_line'][-1][0] < r_base_rclose['best_line'][-1][0] - 0.03, "Center bias trajectory should shift LEFT (-X)"

# Case B: Left wall close (-0.22m), right wall far (+0.78m) -> center_bias should steer RIGHT (-steer, +X)
_populate_walls(cm, x_left=-0.22, x_right=0.78)
mpc.set_center_bias(0.0)
mpc.reset_active_target()
for _ in range(3):
    r_base_lclose = mpc.plan(xg=0.0, yg=1.2, theta_g=0.0, aperture_width=0.8, v_current=1.5, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
mpc.set_center_bias(1.0)
mpc.reset_active_target()
for _ in range(3):
    r_cent_lclose = mpc.plan(xg=0.0, yg=1.2, theta_g=0.0, aperture_width=0.8, v_current=1.5, steer_current=0.0, yaw_rate_current=0.0)
print(
    f"Case B (left close):  bias=0 steer={np.degrees(r_base_lclose['cmd_steer_rad']):+5.2f} deg end_x={r_base_lclose['best_line'][-1][0]:+.3f}m | "
    f"bias=1 steer={np.degrees(r_cent_lclose['cmd_steer_rad']):+5.2f} deg end_x={r_cent_lclose['best_line'][-1][0]:+.3f}m"
)
assert r_cent_lclose['cmd_steer_rad'] < r_base_lclose['cmd_steer_rad'] - 0.004, "Center bias should steer further RIGHT away from close left wall"
assert r_cent_lclose['cmd_steer_rad'] < 0.0, "Center bias should produce negative (RIGHT) steer when left wall is close"
assert r_cent_lclose['best_line'][-1][0] > r_base_lclose['best_line'][-1][0] + 0.03, "Center bias trajectory should shift RIGHT (+X)"

mpc.set_center_bias(0.0)
cm.clear()
mpc.reset_active_target()

print("\n" + "=" * 80)
print("PERFORMANCE BENCHMARK: 100 CONSECUTIVE RUNS")
print("=" * 80)
latencies = []
for _ in range(100):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    mpc.plan(xg=0.4, yg=1.3, theta_g=0.3, aperture_width=0.5, v_current=2.5, steer_current=0.05, yaw_rate_current=0.1)
    latencies.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)

latencies = np.array(latencies)
print(f"Latency: Mean = {latencies.mean():.2f} ms | Min = {latencies.min():.2f} ms | Max = {latencies.max():.2f} ms")
print("\nC++ CURVILINEAR S-L MPC VALIDATION COMPLETE AND PASSED!")


=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/tof_costmap_validator.py ===
import csv
import math
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

# ==============================================================================
# ==============================================================================
# SENSOR RIG DEFINITIONS (16-Channel Front-Only Rig from only_front_tofs.proto)
# Robot Body Frame: +Y is Forward, +X is Right, -X is Left
# ==============================================================================
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToFSensor:
    name: str
    x0: float
    y0: float
    heading_rad: float
    fov_rad: float = 0.19634954  # 11.25 degrees
    min_range: float = 0.001
    max_range: float = 2.40

SENSOR_RIG = [
    # Front-Left Array (cross-pointing Right, rotated 3 deg outwards)
    # Leftmost ToF (pointing straight and right, zones 1.1 deg to 34.9 deg)
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_4.1deg",  -0.058, 0.092, 1.5512),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_15.4deg", -0.058, 0.092, 1.3548),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_26.6deg", -0.058, 0.092, 1.1585),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_37.9deg", -0.058, 0.092, 0.9621),
    # Left ToF (pointing right, zones 46.1 deg to 79.9 deg)
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_49.1deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.7658),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_60.4deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.5694),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_71.6deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.3731),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_left_82.9deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.1767),
    # Front-Right Array (cross-pointing Left, mirrored along Y axis, rotated 3 deg outwards)
    # Rightmost ToF (pointing straight and left, zones 1.1 deg to 34.9 deg)
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_4.1deg",   0.058, 0.092, 1.5904),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_15.4deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.7868),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_26.6deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.9831),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_37.9deg",  0.058, 0.092, 2.1795),
    # Right ToF (pointing left, zones 46.1 deg to 79.9 deg)
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_49.1deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.3758),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_60.4deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.5722),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_71.6deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.7685),
    ToFSensor("tof_front_right_82.9deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.9649),
]


# ==============================================================================
# COSTMAP GENERATOR (Wrapper over C++ DLL or Python Implementation)
# ==============================================================================
class CppCostmapAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        grid_bounds: Tuple[float, float, float, float] = (-1.25, 1.25, 0.0, 2.60),
        resolution: float = 0.015,
        r_inflation_m: float = 0.075,
    ):
        from cpp_smart_hybrid_costmap import CppSmartHybridCostmap
        self.engine = CppSmartHybridCostmap(
            grid_bounds=grid_bounds,
            resolution=resolution,
            r_inflation_m=r_inflation_m,
        )
        self.X = self.engine.X
        self.Y = self.engine.Y
        self.res = resolution

    def build_costmap(self, readings: Dict[str, float], noise_pct: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
        prob_map, _, _ = self.engine.process_tof_frame(readings, noise_pct=noise_pct)
        return prob_map


# ==============================================================================
# GEOMETRY HELPERS FOR PILLARS (CYLINDERS) & CUBES (BOXES)
# ==============================================================================
def compute_surface_error(
    x_est: float,
    y_est: float,
    x_gt: float,
    y_gt: float,
    shape_type: Literal["pillar", "cylinder", "cube", "box"],
    half_width_m: float,
) -> float:
    """Computes the Euclidean deviation from estimated centroid to object boundary."""
    dx = abs(x_est - x_gt)
    dy = abs(y_est - y_gt)

    if shape_type in ("pillar", "cylinder"):
        # Cylinder: distance from center to surface is constant radius R
        dist_to_center = math.hypot(x_est - x_gt, y_est - y_gt)
        return abs(dist_to_center - half_width_m)
    else:
        # Cube / Box (aligned): shortest distance to box boundary perimeter
        if dx >= half_width_m and dy >= half_width_m:
            # Diagonal corner region
            return math.hypot(dx - half_width_m, dy - half_width_m)
        elif dx >= half_width_m:
            # Lateral X edge
            return dx - half_width_m
        elif dy >= half_width_m:
            # Lateral Y edge
            return dy - half_width_m
        else:
            # Inside the box: distance to closest boundary edge
            return min(half_width_m - dx, half_width_m - dy)


def check_containment(
    prob_map: np.ndarray,
    X: np.ndarray,
    Y: np.ndarray,
    x_gt: float,
    y_gt: float,
    shape_type: Literal["pillar", "cylinder", "cube", "box"],
    half_width_m: float,
    grid_res: float,
    threshold: float = 0.55,
) -> bool:
    """Verifies if high-probability cells exist inside the physical bounding shape."""
    if shape_type in ("pillar", "cylinder"):
        dist = np.hypot(X - x_gt, Y - y_gt)
        mask = dist <= (half_width_m + grid_res)
    else:
        dx = np.abs(X - x_gt)
        dy = np.abs(Y - y_gt)
        mask = (dx <= (half_width_m + grid_res)) & (dy <= (half_width_m + grid_res))

    return bool(np.any(prob_map[mask] > threshold))


# ==============================================================================
# ANALYTICAL RAY-TRACER (Webots 16-Channel Sensor Rig Matching)
# ==============================================================================
def _intersect_ray_cylinder(
    x0: float, y0: float, angle: float, cx: float, cy: float, radius: float
) -> float:
    dx = math.cos(angle)
    dy = math.sin(angle)
    fx = x0 - cx
    fy = y0 - cy
    a = dx * dx + dy * dy
    b = 2.0 * (fx * dx + fy * dy)
    c = fx * fx + fy * fy - radius * radius
    disc = b * b - 4.0 * a * c
    if disc < 0.0:
        return 2.40
    sqrt_disc = math.sqrt(disc)
    t1 = (-b - sqrt_disc) / (2.0 * a)
    t2 = (-b + sqrt_disc) / (2.0 * a)
    hits = [t for t in (t1, t2) if 0.02 <= t <= 2.40]
    return min(hits) if hits else 2.40


def _intersect_ray_box(
    x0: float, y0: float, angle: float, cx: float, cy: float, half_w: float
) -> float:
    dx = math.cos(angle)
    dy = math.sin(angle)
    x_min, x_max = cx - half_w, cx + half_w
    y_min, y_max = cy - half_w, cy + half_w

    t_min, t_max = 0.02, 2.40
    if abs(dx) > 1e-6:
        tx1 = (x_min - x0) / dx
        tx2 = (x_max - x0) / dx
        t_min = max(t_min, min(tx1, tx2))
        t_max = min(t_max, max(tx1, tx2))
    else:
        if x0 < x_min or x0 > x_max:
            return 2.40

    if abs(dy) > 1e-6:
        ty1 = (y_min - y0) / dy
        ty2 = (y_max - y0) / dy
        t_min = max(t_min, min(ty1, ty2))
        t_max = min(t_max, max(ty1, ty2))
    else:
        if y0 < y_min or y0 > y_max:
            return 2.40

    if t_min <= t_max and t_max >= 0.02:
        return max(t_min, 0.02)
    return 2.40


def generate_simulated_dataset(
    output_csv: str,
    shape_type: Literal["pillar", "cube"],
    size_m: float,
    grid_step_m: Optional[float] = None,
    x_range: Tuple[float, float] = (-0.925, 0.925),
    y_range: Tuple[float, float] = (0.150, 2.400),
    num_sub_rays: int = 10,
):
    """Generates synthetic ToF range readings with exact Webots optical characteristics."""
    if grid_step_m is None:
        grid_step_m = size_m / 2.0  # Grid size = half width of object

    half_w = size_m / 2.0
    x_points = np.arange(x_range[0], x_range[1] + grid_step_m / 2.0, grid_step_m)
    y_points = np.arange(y_range[0], y_range[1] + grid_step_m / 2.0, grid_step_m)

    sensor_names = [s.name for s in SENSOR_RIG]
    records = []

    for x in x_points:
        for y in y_points:
            row = {"x_m": round(float(x), 4), "y_m": round(float(y), 4)}
            for s in SENSOR_RIG:
                sub_angles = np.linspace(
                    s.heading_rad - s.fov_rad / 2.0,
                    s.heading_rad + s.fov_rad / 2.0,
                    num_sub_rays,
                )
                if shape_type == "pillar":
                    ray_dists = [
                        _intersect_ray_cylinder(s.x0, s.y0, a, float(x), float(y), half_w)
                        for a in sub_angles
                    ]
                else:
                    ray_dists = [
                        _intersect_ray_box(s.x0, s.y0, a, float(x), float(y), half_w)
                        for a in sub_angles
                    ]
                min_dist = min(ray_dists)
                row[s.name] = round(float(min_dist), 4)
            records.append(row)

    with open(output_csv, mode="w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=["x_m", "y_m", *sensor_names])
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(records)

    return output_csv


# ==============================================================================
# FACT-CHECKING ENGINE
# ==============================================================================
def fact_check_dataset(
    csv_input: str,
    csv_output: str,
    shape_type: Literal["pillar", "cylinder", "cube", "box"] = "pillar",
    size_m: float = 0.05,
    grid_resolution_m: Optional[float] = None,
    noise_pct: float = 0.05,
    sigma_front: float = 0.5,
    sigma_back: float = 1.5,
) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Evaluates ToF costmap against ground-truth coordinates with radius/box compensation and overextension checks."""
    half_w = size_m / 2.0
    res = grid_resolution_m if grid_resolution_m is not None else (size_m / 2.0)

    engine = CppCostmapAdapter(grid_bounds=(-1.25, 1.25, 0.0, 2.0), resolution=0.015, r_inflation_m=0.075)

    with open(csv_input, mode="r") as f:
        records = list(csv.DictReader(f))

    sensor_keys = [s.name for s in SENSOR_RIG]
    results = []
    errors = []
    contained_count = 0
    total_detected = 0

    close_overexts = []
    med_overexts = []
    far_overexts = []

    for row in records:
        x_gt = float(row["x_m"])
        y_gt = float(row["y_m"])

        readings = {k: float(row[k]) for k in sensor_keys if k in row}
        active_sensors = [k for k, v in readings.items() if v < 2.39]

        if not active_sensors:
            continue

        total_detected += 1
        prob_map = engine.build_costmap(readings, noise_pct=noise_pct)

        # Filter strictly positive evidence (> 0.55 / log-odds > 0.2)
        hit_cells = prob_map > 0.55
        if not np.any(hit_cells):
            results.append({
                "x_gt_m": x_gt,
                "y_gt_m": y_gt,
                "x_est_surf_m": "NaN",
                "y_est_surf_m": "NaN",
                "surface_error_m": "NaN",
                "front_overext_m": "NaN",
                "range_band": "NaN",
                "active_sensors": len(active_sensors),
                "pillar_contained": False,
                "status": "FALSE_NEGATIVE",
            })
            continue

        # Compute estimated surface centroid
        weights = prob_map[hit_cells]
        x_est_surf = float(np.sum(engine.X[hit_cells] * weights) / np.sum(weights))
        y_est_surf = float(np.sum(engine.Y[hit_cells] * weights) / np.sum(weights))

        # Geometric boundary deviation error
        surface_error = compute_surface_error(
            x_est_surf, y_est_surf, x_gt, y_gt, shape_type, half_w
        )
        errors.append(surface_error)

        # Frontal Overextension towards robot origin (0, 0)
        r_hit_cells = np.hypot(engine.X[hit_cells], engine.Y[hit_cells])
        min_r_occ = float(np.min(r_hit_cells))
        r_gt_center = math.hypot(x_gt, y_gt)
        r_true_front = max(0.0, r_gt_center - half_w)
        front_overextension = max(0.0, r_true_front - min_r_occ)

        # Range categorization
        if r_gt_center < 0.6:
            range_band = "CLOSE"
            close_overexts.append(front_overextension)
        elif r_gt_center < 1.2:
            range_band = "MEDIUM"
            med_overexts.append(front_overextension)
        else:
            range_band = "FAR"
            far_overexts.append(front_overextension)

        # Bounding container overlap test
        is_contained = check_containment(
            prob_map, engine.X, engine.Y, x_gt, y_gt, shape_type, half_w, engine.res
        )
        if is_contained:
            contained_count += 1

        results.append({
            "x_gt_m": round(x_gt, 4),
            "y_gt_m": round(y_gt, 4),
            "x_est_surf_m": round(x_est_surf, 4),
            "y_est_surf_m": round(y_est_surf, 4),
            "surface_error_m": round(surface_error, 4),
            "front_overext_m": round(front_overextension, 4),
            "range_band": range_band,
            "active_sensors": len(active_sensors),
            "pillar_contained": is_contained,
            "status": "OK" if surface_error <= max(0.05, res) else "HIGH_ERROR",
        })

    with open(csv_output, mode="w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=results[0].keys())
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    stats = {
        "total_positions": len(records),
        "total_detected": total_detected,
        "containment_rate": (contained_count / total_detected * 100.0) if total_detected else 0.0,
        "mean_error_cm": float(np.mean(errors) * 100.0) if errors else 0.0,
        "median_error_cm": float(np.median(errors) * 100.0) if errors else 0.0,
        "close_overext_mean_cm": float(np.mean(close_overexts) * 100.0) if close_overexts else 0.0,
        "close_overext_max_cm": float(np.max(close_overexts) * 100.0) if close_overexts else 0.0,
        "med_overext_mean_cm": float(np.mean(med_overexts) * 100.0) if med_overexts else 0.0,
        "far_overext_mean_cm": float(np.mean(far_overexts) * 100.0) if far_overexts else 0.0,
        "ok_rate": (sum(1 for e in errors if e <= max(0.05, res)) / len(errors) * 100.0) if errors else 0.0,
    }

    return stats


# ==============================================================================
# BENCHMARK SUITE: 5, 10, 15, 20 cm Pillars & Cubes
# ==============================================================================
def run_all_benchmarks():
    script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    datasets_dir = os.path.join(script_dir, "data", "datasets")
    reports_dir = os.path.join(script_dir, "data", "reports")
    os.makedirs(datasets_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(reports_dir, exist_ok=True)

    test_configs = [
        # (Shape, Size in meters, Label)
        ("pillar", 0.05, "5 cm Pillar (Dia 5 cm, Grid 2.5 cm)"),
        ("cube",   0.05, "5 cm Cube (Width 5 cm, Grid 2.5 cm)"),
        ("pillar", 0.10, "10 cm Pillar (Dia 10 cm, Grid 5.0 cm)"),
        ("cube",   0.10, "10 cm Cube (Width 10 cm, Grid 5.0 cm)"),
        ("pillar", 0.15, "15 cm Pillar (Dia 15 cm, Grid 7.5 cm)"),
        ("cube",   0.15, "15 cm Cube (Width 15 cm, Grid 7.5 cm)"),
        ("pillar", 0.20, "20 cm Pillar (Dia 20 cm, Grid 10.0 cm)"),
        ("cube",   0.20, "20 cm Cube (Width 20 cm, Grid 10.0 cm)"),
    ]

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("RUNNING MULTI-OBJECT TOF COSTMAP BENCHMARK SUITE")
    print("=" * 80)

    summary_rows = []

    for shape, size, label in test_configs:
        grid_res = size / 2.0  # Grid size is half width of object
        input_csv = os.path.join(datasets_dir, f"tof_readings_{shape}_{int(size*100)}cm.csv")
        output_csv = os.path.join(reports_dir, f"tof_validation_{shape}_{int(size*100)}cm.csv")

        # Generate dataset if it's new, or reuse existing 5cm pillar dataset if present
        legacy_5cm_csv = os.path.join(datasets_dir, "tof_16ch_grid_readings.csv")
        if shape == "pillar" and size == 0.05 and os.path.exists(legacy_5cm_csv):
            dataset_file = legacy_5cm_csv
        elif os.path.exists(input_csv):
            dataset_file = input_csv
        else:
            dataset_file = generate_simulated_dataset(
                output_csv=input_csv,
                shape_type=shape,
                size_m=size,
                grid_step_m=grid_res,
            )

        stats = fact_check_dataset(
            csv_input=dataset_file,
            csv_output=output_csv,
            shape_type=shape,
            size_m=size,
            grid_resolution_m=grid_res,
            noise_pct=0.05,
        )

        summary_rows.append((label, shape, size, grid_res, stats))
        print(f"[{label}] -> Containment: {stats['containment_rate']:.1f}%, Mean Err: {stats['mean_error_cm']:.2f} cm, Close Overext (Front): {stats['close_overext_mean_cm']:.2f} cm (Max: {stats['close_overext_max_cm']:.2f} cm), Med Overext: {stats['med_overext_mean_cm']:.2f} cm")

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("BENCHMARK EXECUTION COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 80 + "\n")
    return summary_rows


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all_benchmarks()


=== FILE: 5_webots/simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/tof_gap_scout.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  16-CHANNEL TOF & 15mm COSTMAP MULTI-TARGET GAP SCOUT MODULE

  Key Upgrades:
  1. RAYTRACING ON INFLATED COSTMAP (Hazard Mask):
     Directly casts radial lines across the front FOV through the expanded
     hazard grid (15mm resolution, dilated for vehicle clearance).
     For each line, computes the exact unobstructed distance to the first obstacle.
  2. MULTI-TARGET DISCOVERY:
     Finds the top local maxima (distinct furthest clear targets) so the vehicle
     is not stuck to a single centerline point.
  3. APERTURE & HEADING ESTIMATION:
     Provides primary target (xg, yg, width, theta) plus alternative targets
     so the trajectory planners evaluate candidates toward multiple openings.
================================================================================
"""

import math
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np

try:
    from .planner_config import (
        GAP_MIN_WIDTH_M,
        GAP_MIN_LOOKAHEAD_M,
        GAP_MAX_LOOKAHEAD_M,
        GRID_X_MIN,
        GRID_X_MAX,
        GRID_Y_MIN,
        GRID_Y_MAX,
        GRID_RES,
        TOF_MAX_RANGE_M,
        R_INFLATION_M,
        WEIGHT_GAP_DISTANCE,
        WEIGHT_GAP_WIDTH,
        WEIGHT_ALIGN_STEER,
        WEIGHT_ALIGN_STRAIGHT,
        WEIGHT_SIDE_CLEARANCE,
        TRACK_CORRIDOR_WIDTH_M,
        GAP_DIST_THRESH_M,
        GAP_DIST_EXP_K,
        ALT_MIN_SEPARATION_M,
        ALT_APERTURE_BIAS,
    )
except ImportError:
    from planner_config import (
        GAP_MIN_WIDTH_M,
        GAP_MIN_LOOKAHEAD_M,
        GAP_MAX_LOOKAHEAD_M,
        GRID_X_MIN,
        GRID_X_MAX,
        GRID_Y_MIN,
        GRID_Y_MAX,
        GRID_RES,
        TOF_MAX_RANGE_M,
        R_INFLATION_M,
        WEIGHT_GAP_DISTANCE,
        WEIGHT_GAP_WIDTH,
        WEIGHT_ALIGN_STEER,
        WEIGHT_ALIGN_STRAIGHT,
        WEIGHT_SIDE_CLEARANCE,
        TRACK_CORRIDOR_WIDTH_M,
        GAP_DIST_THRESH_M,
        GAP_DIST_EXP_K,
        ALT_MIN_SEPARATION_M,
        ALT_APERTURE_BIAS,
    )

# 16-Channel Physical ToF Sensor Mounting Poses
# Format: (sensor_name, mount_x, mount_y, heading_rad, max_range_m)
FRONT_TOF_SENSOR_RIG = [
    # Front-Left Array (cross-pointing Right, rotated 3 deg outwards)
    # Leftmost ToF (pointing straight and right, zones 1.1 deg to 34.9 deg)
    ("tof_front_left_4.1deg",  -0.058, 0.092, 1.5512, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_15.4deg", -0.058, 0.092, 1.3548, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_26.6deg", -0.058, 0.092, 1.1585, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_37.9deg", -0.058, 0.092, 0.9621, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    # Left ToF (pointing right, zones 46.1 deg to 79.9 deg)
    ("tof_front_left_49.1deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.7658, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_60.4deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.5694, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_71.6deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.3731, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_left_82.9deg", -0.057, 0.090, 0.1767, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    # Front-Right Array (cross-pointing Left, mirrored along Y axis, rotated 3 deg outwards)
    # Rightmost ToF (pointing straight and left, zones 1.1 deg to 34.9 deg)
    ("tof_front_right_4.1deg",   0.058, 0.092, 1.5904, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_15.4deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.7868, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_26.6deg",  0.058, 0.092, 1.9831, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_37.9deg",  0.058, 0.092, 2.1795, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    # Right ToF (pointing left, zones 46.1 deg to 79.9 deg)
    ("tof_front_right_49.1deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.3758, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_60.4deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.5722, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_71.6deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.7685, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
    ("tof_front_right_82.9deg",  0.057, 0.090, 2.9649, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M),
]


# 16-Channel ToF Pod Geometry:
# 8 zones per corner, each zone has 11.25 deg FOV (8 * 11.25 deg = 90.0 deg total FOV per pod).
# Zone midpoints: 1.125 deg, 12.375 deg, ..., 79.875 deg.
# Zone outer bounds: from -4.5 deg (-0.0785 rad) to +85.5 deg (+1.4923 rad). Outer edges meet at 171 deg.
_NUM_CORNER_RAYS = 90

# Left Pod (-0.0575m, 0.091m, cross-pointing Right): -4.5 deg to +85.5 deg rel to +Y
_LEFT_REL_ANGLES = np.linspace(math.radians(-4.5), math.radians(85.5), _NUM_CORNER_RAYS)
_LEFT_AZIMUTHS = (math.pi / 2.0) - _LEFT_REL_ANGLES
_LEFT_COS = np.cos(_LEFT_AZIMUTHS)
_LEFT_SIN = np.sin(_LEFT_AZIMUTHS)

# Right Pod (+0.0575m, 0.091m, cross-pointing Left): +4.5 deg to -85.5 deg rel to +Y (i.e. -4.5 deg to +85.5 deg leftwards)
_RIGHT_REL_ANGLES = np.linspace(math.radians(4.5), -math.radians(85.5), _NUM_CORNER_RAYS)
_RIGHT_AZIMUTHS = (math.pi / 2.0) - _RIGHT_REL_ANGLES
_RIGHT_COS = np.cos(_RIGHT_AZIMUTHS)
_RIGHT_SIN = np.sin(_RIGHT_AZIMUTHS)


class ToFGapScout:
    """
    Scouts furthest points and navigable openings by combining direct ToF sensor
    beam sightlines (LUT) with C++ inflated obstacle raycasting.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        w_gap_distance: float = WEIGHT_GAP_DISTANCE,
        w_gap_width: float = WEIGHT_GAP_WIDTH,
        w_align_steer: float = WEIGHT_ALIGN_STEER,
        w_align_straight: float = WEIGHT_ALIGN_STRAIGHT,
        w_side_clearance: float = WEIGHT_SIDE_CLEARANCE,
        gap_dist_exp_k: float = GAP_DIST_EXP_K,
        gap_dist_thresh_m: float = GAP_DIST_THRESH_M,
        gap_progress_margin_m: Optional[float] = None,
        alt_min_separation_m: float = ALT_MIN_SEPARATION_M,
        alt_aperture_bias: float = ALT_APERTURE_BIAS,
        scout_obs_width_m: float = 0.22,
        scout_block_dist_m: Optional[float] = None,
        scout_straight_pen_k: float = 2.50,
        scout_straight_exp_k: float = 1.50,
        ray_origin_offset_m: float = 0.0575,
        w_gap_clear: float = 3.00,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self.w_gap_distance = float(w_gap_distance)
        self.w_gap_width = float(w_gap_width)
        self.w_align_steer = float(w_align_steer)
        self.w_align_straight = float(w_align_straight)
        self.w_side_clearance = float(w_side_clearance)
        self.gap_dist_exp_k = float(gap_dist_exp_k)
        if gap_progress_margin_m is not None:
            self.gap_dist_thresh_m = float(TRACK_CORRIDOR_WIDTH_M + gap_progress_margin_m)
        else:
            self.gap_dist_thresh_m = float(gap_dist_thresh_m)
        self.alt_min_separation_m = float(alt_min_separation_m)
        self.alt_aperture_bias = float(alt_aperture_bias)
        self.scout_obs_width_m = float(scout_obs_width_m)
        self.scout_block_dist_m = float(scout_block_dist_m) if scout_block_dist_m is not None else None
        self.scout_straight_pen_k = float(scout_straight_pen_k)
        self.scout_straight_exp_k = float(scout_straight_exp_k)
        self.ray_origin_offset_m = float(ray_origin_offset_m)
        self.w_gap_clear = float(kwargs.get("alt_lookahead_depth_weight", w_gap_clear))
        self.last_xg: float = 0.0
        self.last_yg: float = 1.20
        self.last_aperture: float = 0.50
        self.last_targets: List[Tuple[float, float, float]] = []

    def scout_gap(
        self,
        tof_readings: Dict[str, float],
        hazard_mask: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        costmap_obj: Optional[any] = None,
        target_lookahead_m: float = 2.30,
        steer_current: float = 0.0,
        prob_map: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        anchor_x: float = 0.0,
        anchor_y: float = 0.0,
        anchor_yaw: float = 0.0,
    ) -> Tuple[float, float, float, float]:
        """
        Returns primary (x_g, y_g, aperture_width_m, recovery_heading_rad).
        Also stores multi-target options in self.last_targets: [(xg, yg, score), ...].
        """
        targets = self.find_furthest_targets(
            tof_readings=tof_readings,
            hazard_mask=hazard_mask,
            costmap_obj=costmap_obj,
            max_lookahead_m=target_lookahead_m,
            top_k=2,
            steer_current=steer_current,
            anchor_x=anchor_x,
            anchor_y=anchor_y,
            anchor_yaw=anchor_yaw,
        )

        if not targets:
            xg_prim = anchor_x
            yg_prim = anchor_y + max(GAP_MIN_LOOKAHEAD_M, min(target_lookahead_m, GAP_MAX_LOOKAHEAD_M))
            aperture = 0.45
        else:
            xg_prim, yg_prim, aperture = targets[0][:3]

        # Raw physical target coordinates directly without EMA smoothing or midpoint blending
        self.last_xg = xg_prim
        self.last_yg = yg_prim
        self.last_aperture = aperture
        self.last_targets = targets

        raw_rel = math.atan2(xg_prim - anchor_x, max(0.20, yg_prim - anchor_y)) + anchor_yaw
        theta_g = math.atan2(math.sin(raw_rel), math.cos(raw_rel))
        return xg_prim, yg_prim, aperture, theta_g

    def find_furthest_targets(
        self,
        tof_readings: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
        hazard_mask: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        costmap_obj: Optional[any] = None,
        max_lookahead_m: float = 1.40,
        top_k: int = 3,
        steer_current: float = 0.0,
        prob_map: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        anchor_x: float = 0.0,
        anchor_y: float = 0.0,
        anchor_yaw: float = 0.0,
    ) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
        """
        Extracts furthest targets by evaluating real ToF beam sightlines and dense radial
        rays through the C++ inflated costmap (hazard_mask).
        Guarantees targets lie on physical clear space at the true lookahead distance
        without colliding with inflated obstacle boundaries.
        Returns list of (x, y, aperture_width).
        """
        # Fast C++ Native Gap Scout Path (0.05 ms vs 5 ms)
        if costmap_obj is not None and hasattr(costmap_obj, "scout_gaps_native") and hasattr(costmap_obj, "_handle") and costmap_obj._handle:
            targets = costmap_obj.scout_gaps_native(
                max_lookahead_m=max_lookahead_m,
                steer_current=steer_current,
                w_gap_distance=self.w_gap_distance,
                w_gap_width=self.w_gap_width,
                w_align_steer=self.w_align_steer,
                w_align_straight=self.w_align_straight,
                w_side_clearance=self.w_side_clearance,
                gap_dist_exp_k=self.gap_dist_exp_k,
                gap_dist_thresh_m=self.gap_dist_thresh_m,
                alt_min_separation_m=self.alt_min_separation_m,
                alt_aperture_bias=self.alt_aperture_bias,
                scout_obs_width_m=self.scout_obs_width_m,
                scout_block_dist_m=self.scout_block_dist_m if self.scout_block_dist_m is not None else max_lookahead_m,
                scout_straight_pen_k=self.scout_straight_pen_k,
                scout_straight_exp_k=self.scout_straight_exp_k,
                top_k=top_k,
                hazard_mask=hazard_mask,
                anchor_x=anchor_x,
                anchor_y=anchor_y,
                anchor_yaw=anchor_yaw,
            )
            return targets

        if hazard_mask is None or costmap_obj is None:
            return [(0.0, max_lookahead_m, 0.40)]

        res = costmap_obj.res
        x_min = costmap_obj.x_min
        y_min = costmap_obj.y_min
        h, w = hazard_mask.shape
        step_ds = max(res * 0.8, 0.012)

        candidates = []

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. EVALUATE 16 PHYSICAL TOF BEAM SIGHTLINES (LUT + MEASURED RANGES)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        if tof_readings:
            active_rig = FRONT_TOF_SENSOR_RIG
            for s_name, sx, sy, heading, max_range in active_rig:
                raw_z = tof_readings.get(s_name, max_range)
                z = min(max_range, max(0.10, raw_z))
                cos_h = math.cos(heading)
                sin_h = math.sin(heading)

                clear_dist = z
                hit_obs = False
                n_steps = int(z / step_ds)
                for s in range(1, n_steps + 1):
                    d = s * step_ds
                    px = sx + d * cos_h
                    py = sy + d * sin_h
                    col = int(round((px - x_min) / res))
                    row = int(round((py - y_min) / res))
                    if row < 0 or row >= h or col < 0 or col >= w:
                        clear_dist = max(0.0, d - step_ds)
                        break
                    if hazard_mask[row, col] > 0:
                        clear_dist = max(0.0, d - step_ds)
                        hit_obs = True
                        break

                # Target at maximum clear beam distance (backed off by 7.5cm safety margin ONLY if obstacle hit)
                eff_dist = max(0.05, clear_dist - 0.075) if hit_obs else clear_dist
                tx = sx + eff_dist * cos_h
                ty = max(0.08, sy + eff_dist * sin_h)
                d_ax = tx - anchor_x
                d_ay = max(0.05, ty - anchor_y)
                total_dist = math.hypot(d_ax, d_ay)

                # Heading in steering coordinates relative to anchor (positive = left)
                raw_rel = math.atan2(d_ax, d_ay) + anchor_yaw
                rel_angle = math.atan2(math.sin(raw_rel), math.cos(raw_rel))
                steer_equiv = -rel_angle

                # Aperture estimation around this beam
                aperture_m = max(GAP_MIN_WIDTH_M, min(1.20, total_dist * 0.35))
                is_clear = (not hit_obs) and (raw_z >= (max_range - 0.01) or total_dist >= (max_lookahead_m - 0.05))
                candidates.append({
                    "tx": tx, "ty": ty, "dist": total_dist,
                    "aperture": aperture_m, "steer_equiv": steer_equiv,
                    "rel_angle": rel_angle,
                    "is_clear": is_clear,
                })

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. EVALUATE DENSE RADIAL RAYS (180 TOTAL: 90 PER FRONT CORNER POD)
        # Left Corner (-0.07m, 0.095m):  90 rays spanning -89 deg to 0 deg (Left to Center)
        # Right Corner (+0.07m, 0.095m): 90 rays spanning 0 deg to +89 deg (Center to Right)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        max_r = min(max_lookahead_m, GAP_MAX_LOOKAHEAD_M)
        n_steps = int(max_r / step_ds)

        corner_fans = [
            (-self.ray_origin_offset_m, 0.091, _LEFT_COS, _LEFT_SIN),
            (self.ray_origin_offset_m, 0.091, _RIGHT_COS, _RIGHT_SIN),
        ]

        for ox, oy, cos_arr, sin_arr in corner_fans:
            for i in range(_NUM_CORNER_RAYS):
                cos_az = float(cos_arr[i])
                sin_az = float(sin_arr[i])
                clear_dist = max_r
                hit_obs = False

                for s in range(1, n_steps + 1):
                    d = s * step_ds
                    px = ox + d * cos_az
                    py = oy + d * sin_az
                    col = int(round((px - x_min) / res))
                    row = int(round((py - y_min) / res))
                    if row < 0 or row >= h or col < 0 or col >= w:
                        clear_dist = max(0.0, d - step_ds)
                        break
                    if hazard_mask[row, col] > 0:
                        clear_dist = max(0.0, d - step_ds)
                        hit_obs = True
                        break

                eff_dist = max(0.05, clear_dist - 0.06) if hit_obs else clear_dist
                # Physical target point in vehicle local coordinates (origin at vehicle center / rear axle)
                tx = ox + eff_dist * cos_az
                ty = max(0.08, oy + eff_dist * sin_az)
                d_ax = tx - anchor_x
                d_ay = max(0.05, ty - anchor_y)
                total_dist = math.hypot(d_ax, d_ay)

                # True relative angle from anchor to target (tx, ty)
                raw_rel = math.atan2(d_ax, d_ay) + anchor_yaw
                rel_angle = math.atan2(math.sin(raw_rel), math.cos(raw_rel))
                steer_equiv = -rel_angle
                aperture_m = max(GAP_MIN_WIDTH_M, min(1.20, total_dist * 0.30))

                candidates.append({
                    "tx": tx, "ty": ty, "dist": total_dist,
                    "aperture": aperture_m, "steer_equiv": steer_equiv,
                    "rel_angle": rel_angle,
                    "is_clear": not hit_obs,
                })

        if not candidates:
            return [(anchor_x, anchor_y + max_lookahead_m, 0.40, True)]

        max_clear_dist = max([c["dist"] for c in candidates])
        has_far_path = max_clear_dist > 0.85
        min_allowed_dist = max(0.30, max_clear_dist * 0.45) if has_far_path else 0.30

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. SCORE CANDIDATE TARGETS
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Check forward clearance directly along vehicle forward axis from anchor
        d_center = max_clear_dist
        if hazard_mask is not None:
            n_center_steps = int(max_r / step_ds)
            cos_fwd = -math.sin(anchor_yaw)
            sin_fwd =  math.cos(anchor_yaw)
            for s in range(1, n_center_steps + 1):
                d = s * step_ds
                px = anchor_x + d * cos_fwd
                py = anchor_y + 0.091 + d * sin_fwd
                col = int(round((px - x_min) / res))
                row = int(round((py - y_min) / res))
                if row < 0 or row >= h or col < 0 or col >= w or hazard_mask[row, col] > 0:
                    d_center = max(0.0, d - step_ds)
                    break
            else:
                d_center = max_r
        else:
            center_cands = [c["dist"] for c in candidates if abs(c["rel_angle"]) < math.radians(6)]
            d_center = max(center_cands) if center_cands else max_clear_dist

        # Compute side clearance asymmetry from lateral and front ToF sensors (8 per side):
        side_diff = 0.0
        if tof_readings:
            if any("front" in k for k in tof_readings):
                # 16-channel array:
                # Left-side openness: left front 4.1deg + right pod cross-pointing left 15.4deg to 82.9deg
                left_keys = ["tof_front_left_4.1deg"] + [f"tof_front_right_{ang}deg" for ang in [15.4, 26.6, 37.9, 49.1, 60.4, 71.6, 82.9]]
                # Right-side openness: right front 4.1deg + left pod cross-pointing right 15.4deg to 82.9deg
                right_keys = ["tof_front_right_4.1deg"] + [f"tof_front_left_{ang}deg" for ang in [15.4, 26.6, 37.9, 49.1, 60.4, 71.6, 82.9]]
            else:
                left_keys = ["tof_fl_3", "tof_fr_0", "tof_fr_1", "tof_fr_2", "tof_bl_0", "tof_bl_1", "tof_bl_2", "tof_bl_3"]
                right_keys = ["tof_fr_3", "tof_fl_0", "tof_fl_1", "tof_fl_2", "tof_br_0", "tof_br_1", "tof_br_2", "tof_br_3"]

            l_vals = [tof_readings.get(k, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M) for k in left_keys]
            r_vals = [tof_readings.get(k, TOF_MAX_RANGE_M) for k in right_keys]
            l_total = sum(l_vals) / len(l_vals) if l_vals else TOF_MAX_RANGE_M
            r_total = sum(r_vals) / len(r_vals) if r_vals else TOF_MAX_RANGE_M
            denom = max(0.25, 0.5 * (l_total + r_total))
            side_diff = (l_total - r_total) / denom

        w_straight = self.w_align_straight
        straight_block_penalty = 0.0
        theta_shadow = 0.0
        eff_block_dist = self.scout_block_dist_m if self.scout_block_dist_m is not None else max_r
        if d_center < eff_block_dist:
            theta_shadow = math.atan2(self.scout_obs_width_m * 0.5, max(d_center, 0.05))
            straight_block_penalty = self.scout_straight_pen_k * max(0.0, (eff_block_dist - d_center) / eff_block_dist)

        # Relax steer alignment penalty when facing an obstacle or turn
        w_steer = self.w_align_steer if (abs(steer_current) > 0.08 and d_center >= 1.0) else 0.20

        scored_candidates = []
        for c in candidates:
            if c["dist"] < min_allowed_dist:
                continue

            d_cand = c["dist"]
            d_thresh = self.gap_dist_thresh_m
            dist_base = d_cand / max(max_clear_dist, 0.10)
            if d_cand > d_thresh:
                # Slight exponential boost for distances exceeding threshold (open track corridor towards progress)
                excess = d_cand - d_thresh
                dist_factor = dist_base * math.exp(self.gap_dist_exp_k * excess)
            else:
                dist_factor = dist_base

            width_norm = c["aperture"] / 1.00
            steer_diff = abs(c["steer_equiv"] - steer_current)
            straight_diff = abs(c["rel_angle"])

            # Neighboring ToF Zone Width Bonus (0.3x lowest cost / best neighboring zone clearance)
            neighbor_bonus = 0.0
            if tof_readings:
                # Find matching physical zone index for candidate angle
                rel_deg = math.degrees(c["rel_angle"])
                active_rig = FRONT_TOF_SENSOR_RIG
                if rel_deg >= 0.0:
                    z_idx = min(7, max(0, int(round((rel_deg - 1.125) / 11.25))))
                else:
                    z_idx = min(15, max(8, 8 + int(round((-rel_deg - 1.125) / 11.25))))
                
                left_neighbor_dist = tof_readings.get(active_rig[z_idx - 1][0], 0.0) if (z_idx > 0 and z_idx != 8) else 0.0
                right_neighbor_dist = tof_readings.get(active_rig[z_idx + 1][0], 0.0) if (z_idx < 15 and z_idx != 7) else 0.0
                min_neighbor_dist = max(left_neighbor_dist, right_neighbor_dist)
                neighbor_norm = min(TOF_MAX_RANGE_M, min_neighbor_dist) / TOF_MAX_RANGE_M
                neighbor_bonus = 0.30 * self.w_gap_width * neighbor_norm

            # Side clearance alignment bonus:
            # c["rel_angle"] < 0 is left. -sin(rel_angle) > 0 for left targets.
            # If side_diff > 0 (left has longer reading), left targets receive a strong bonus!
            side_bonus = self.w_side_clearance * side_diff * (-math.sin(c["rel_angle"]))

            # Penalize center/straight targets within dynamic angular shadow cone
            center_penalty = 0.0
            if straight_block_penalty > 0.0 and theta_shadow > 0.001 and abs(c["rel_angle"]) < theta_shadow:
                angle_ratio = 1.0 - (abs(c["rel_angle"]) / theta_shadow)
                center_penalty = straight_block_penalty * math.exp(self.scout_straight_exp_k * angle_ratio)

            clear_bonus = self.w_gap_clear if c.get("is_clear", False) else 0.0

            score = (
                self.w_gap_distance * dist_factor
                + self.w_gap_width * width_norm
                + neighbor_bonus
                + side_bonus
                + clear_bonus
                - center_penalty
                - w_steer * steer_diff
                - w_straight * straight_diff
            )
            scored_candidates.append((score, c))

        scored_candidates.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. SMART DUAL-PATH SELECTION (Primary Racing Line + 1 Smart Challenger)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        targets = []
        if scored_candidates:
            best_score, c0 = scored_candidates[0]
            tx0, ty0, ap0 = c0["tx"], c0["ty"], c0["aperture"]
            targets.append((tx0, ty0, ap0, bool(c0.get("is_clear", False))))

            if top_k >= 2:
                # Target 1: Smart Challenger Line
                # Select the best distinct corridor opening on the opposite side of
                # the obstacle / centerline or separated by at least alt_min_separation_m.
                # The challenger is scored with alt_aperture_bias weighting safe clearance.
                challenger_candidates = []
                for _, c in scored_candidates:
                    tx, ty, ap = c["tx"], c["ty"], c["aperture"]
                    lat_sep = abs(tx - tx0)
                    ang_sep = abs(c["rel_angle"] - c0["rel_angle"])
                    is_opposite_side = (tx0 < -0.04 and tx > 0.04) or (tx0 > 0.04 and tx < -0.04)
                    min_ang_sep = math.radians(24.0)

                    # Must be separated by minimum distance AND not be an immediately adjacent zone
                    if (lat_sep >= self.alt_min_separation_m or is_opposite_side) and (ang_sep >= min_ang_sep or is_opposite_side):
                        # Compute challenger score with aperture bias
                        d_cand = c["dist"]
                        d_thresh = self.gap_dist_thresh_m
                        dist_base = d_cand / max(max_clear_dist, 0.10)
                        if d_cand > d_thresh:
                            dist_factor = dist_base * math.exp(self.gap_dist_exp_k * (d_cand - d_thresh))
                        else:
                            dist_factor = dist_base

                        width_norm = ap / 1.00
                        steer_diff = abs(c["steer_equiv"] - steer_current)
                        straight_diff = abs(c["rel_angle"])
                        side_bonus_alt = (self.w_side_clearance * 0.45) * side_diff * (-math.sin(c["rel_angle"]))
                        clear_bonus_alt = (self.w_gap_clear * self.alt_aperture_bias) if c.get("is_clear", False) else 0.0
                        center_penalty = 0.0
                        if straight_block_penalty > 0.0 and theta_shadow > 0.001 and abs(c["rel_angle"]) < theta_shadow:
                            angle_ratio = 1.0 - (abs(c["rel_angle"]) / theta_shadow)
                            center_penalty = straight_block_penalty * math.exp(self.scout_straight_exp_k * angle_ratio)

                        score_alt = (
                            self.w_gap_distance * dist_factor
                            + (self.w_gap_width * self.alt_aperture_bias) * width_norm
                            + side_bonus_alt
                            + clear_bonus_alt
                            - center_penalty
                            - w_steer * steer_diff
                            - w_straight * straight_diff
                        )
                        challenger_candidates.append((score_alt, c))

                if challenger_candidates:
                    challenger_candidates.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
                    best_challenger = challenger_candidates[0][1]
                    targets.append((best_challenger["tx"], best_challenger["ty"], best_challenger["aperture"], bool(best_challenger.get("is_clear", False))))
                else:
                    # Fallback: next distinct candidate by spatial separation >= 15 cm
                    for _, c in scored_candidates:
                        tx, ty, ap = c["tx"], c["ty"], c["aperture"]
                        if math.hypot(tx - tx0, ty - ty0) >= 0.15:
                            targets.append((tx, ty, ap, bool(c.get("is_clear", False))))
                            break

                # Additional targets if top_k > 2 requested
                if top_k > 2:
                    for _, c in scored_candidates:
                        tx, ty, ap = c["tx"], c["ty"], c["aperture"]
                        if any(math.hypot(tx - ex, ty - ey) < 0.18 for (ex, ey, *_) in targets):
                            continue
                        targets.append((tx, ty, ap, bool(c.get("is_clear", False))))
                        if len(targets) >= top_k:
                            break

        # Fallback if no target met threshold
        if not targets:
            best_c = max(candidates, key=lambda x: x["dist"])
            targets = [(best_c["tx"], best_c["ty"], 0.40, bool(best_c.get("is_clear", False)))]

        return targets
